Тема: № 101 Конфликт с шумными соседями и его решение ⁦(Dealing with noisy neighbors)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎noisy [ˈnɔɪzi] — шумный
2.‎to bang [bæŋ] — стучать, греметь
3.‎to complain [kəmˈpleɪn] — жаловаться
4.‎to knock on the door [nɒk ɒn ðə dɔː] — постучать в дверь
5.‎to lose one's temper [luːz wʌnz ˈtempə] — выйти из себя
6.‎to apologise [əˈpɒlədʒaɪz] — извиниться
7.‎compromise [ˈkɒmprəmaɪz] — компромисс
8.‎headphones [ˈhedfəʊnz] — наушники

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~270 слов.
It was 11:40 PM. The bass from flat 7 was shaking Julia's cup on the table. Again. Third night in a row.
‎"That's it," she said, standing up. "I'm going to knock on that door and lose my temper."
‎Her flatmate, Ben, caught her arm. "Wait. You're angry. Angry conversations at midnight never end well."
‎"I have to be at work at seven!"
‎"I know. So be smart, not loud."
‎Julia took a breath, put on her shoes, and knocked. The music stopped. A young man opened the door, holding a game controller.
‎"Hi," Julia said, forcing a smile. "I'm Julia, from flat 5. Right below you."
‎"Oh. Hey." He looked embarrassed. "Is it too loud?"
‎"It's almost midnight and my walls are shaking. I haven't slept properly since Tuesday."
‎"Ah, man. I'm so sorry. I just got a new sound system and I got carried away. I didn't realise it travelled downstairs."
‎Julia felt her anger disappear. "You really didn't know?"
‎"No idea. I thought these walls were concrete."
‎"They're not. They're basically cardboard."
‎He laughed. "Okay. Look — can we find a compromise? I love playing at night, but I don't want to be that neighbour."
‎"Headphones?" Julia suggested. "Or finish by ten on weekdays?"
‎"Both. Headphones after ten, and no speakers after nine on work nights. Weekends I'll warn you if I have friends over."
‎"That's… actually perfect."
‎"Deal. And if I forget, just text me instead of suffering for three days. I'm Ryan, by the way."
‎The next morning, Julia found a note in her mailbox: "Sorry again. Bought headphones. — Ryan (flat 7)"
‎She showed it to Ben. "You were right. A conversation, not a war."

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Why couldn't Julia sleep for three nights?
2.‎What advice did Ben give her before she knocked on the door?
3.‎What compromise did Julia and Ryan agree on?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, the music from flat 7 was so... ⁦(noisy)⁩ that Julia's cup was shaking.
She wanted to knock on the door and... ⁦(lose her temper)⁩.
Ben told her to be... ⁦(smart)⁩, not loud.
She introduced herself to her neighbour... ⁦(Ryan)⁩.
He was embarrassed and said he didn't... ⁦(realise)⁩ the sound travelled downstairs.
He offered to... ⁦(apologise)⁩ and find a compromise.
They agreed on... ⁦(headphones)⁩ after ten o'clock.
The next morning Julia found a... ⁦(note)⁩ in her mailbox.
She realised a conversation works better than a... ⁦(war)⁩.

Тема: № 102 Поход в супермаркет: составление списка и неожиданные покупки ⁦(Grocery shopping: making a list and impulse buys)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎shopping list [ˈʃɒpɪŋ lɪst] — список покупок
2.‎trolley [ˈtrɒli] — тележка
3.‎aisle [aɪl] — проход между полками, ряд
4.‎special offer [ˈspeʃl ˈɒfə] — специальная акция
5.‎impulse buy [ˈɪmpʌls baɪ] — спонтанная покупка
6.‎receipt [rɪˈsiːt] — чек
7.‎to stick to the list [stɪk tə ðə lɪst] — придерживаться списка
8.‎expired [ɪkˈspaɪəd] — просроченный

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
"Okay," said Nina, writing on a piece of paper. "Milk, eggs, bread, chicken, rice, tomatoes. Forty pounds. That's the budget."
‎Her husband, Sam, took the list. "Six things. How long can it take?"
‎"Twenty minutes."
‎Fifty minutes later, Sam came home with three bags and no chicken.
‎Nina looked inside. "Sam. Where is the chicken?"
‎"Ah. Well. There was a special offer on barbecue sauce."
‎"We don't have a barbecue."
‎"We could get one!"
‎Nina pulled out the receipt. "Sam, this is eighty-three pounds. Eighty-three. For six things."
‎"Technically I bought twenty things."
‎"That's the problem!" She read the list out loud. "Pineapple. Chocolate biscuits. A giant bag of crisps. Sushi. A candle that smells like the sea."
‎"The candle was three for two."
‎"Why do we need three candles that smell like the sea?"
‎Sam sat down, looking guilty. "I went shopping hungry. You always say never do that."
‎"I do say that. I say it every week."
‎"And I ignored the aisle order. I started at the sweets, so by the time I got to the vegetables I had no money and no willpower."
‎Nina started laughing. "You're hopeless."
‎"I know. But look — I did buy milk."
‎She checked. "This milk is expired. It expired yesterday."
‎"Right. So we're having chocolate biscuits for dinner."
‎The next Saturday, Nina took the list back. "New rules. We eat before we go. We stick to the list. And we go together, so I can watch your hands near the offers."
‎"Fair," Sam said. "But can I keep the sea candle?"
‎"You can keep one."

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎What was on the shopping list and what was the budget?
2.‎Why did Sam spend eighty-three pounds instead of forty?
3.‎What new rules did Nina suggest for the next shopping trip?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Nina wrote a... ⁦(shopping list)⁩ with six things and a forty-pound budget.
Sam took a trolley and came home fifty... ⁦(minutes)⁩ later.
He forgot the chicken but bought barbecue sauce on a... ⁦(special offer)⁩.
Nina checked the... ⁦(receipt)⁩ and saw eighty-three pounds.
Most of the extra things were... ⁦(impulse buys)⁩, like crisps and a candle.
Sam admitted he went shopping... ⁦(hungry)⁩ and started in the sweets aisle.
Even the milk he bought was... ⁦(expired)⁩.
Nina made new rules: eat first and... ⁦(stick to the list)⁩.
Sam was allowed to keep one... ⁦(candle)⁩.

Тема: № 103 Генеральная уборка и распределение домашних обязанностей ⁦(Deep cleaning and dividing household chores)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎deep cleaning [diːp ˈkliːnɪŋ] — генеральная уборка
2.‎chore [tʃɔː] — домашняя обязанность
3.‎vacuum cleaner [ˈvækjuːm ˈkliːnə] — пылесос
4.‎to dust [dʌst] — вытирать пыль
5.‎laundry [ˈlɔːndri] — стирка, бельё для стирки
6.‎to take out the rubbish [teɪk aʊt ðə ˈrʌbɪʃ] — выносить мусор
7.‎to scrub [skrʌb] — оттирать, драить
8.‎rota [ˈrəʊtə] — график очерёдности

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~270 слов.
"Look at this kitchen," said Mum, running a finger along the top of the fridge. "This isn't dust. This is archaeology."
‎Fifteen-year-old Ben looked up from his phone. "It's not that bad."
‎"Your father's mug from Tuesday is growing something. We're doing a deep cleaning. Today. All of us."
‎Dad walked in with the vacuum cleaner. "I've been ready since 2019."
‎"Right," Mum said, opening her notebook. "Let's divide the chores properly this time. Not like last month, when I did everything and you two 'helped' for ten minutes."
‎"That's unfair," Ben said.
‎"Is it? Who took out the rubbish last week?"
‎"…Nobody. It's still by the door."
‎"Exactly. So — a rota. A real one, on the fridge."
‎She wrote three columns. "Ben: vacuum your room and the living room, and take out the rubbish every evening."
‎"Every evening?"
‎"It takes ninety seconds. Dad: bathroom. You love scrubbing tiles, you told me."
‎"I said I don't mind it. That's different."
‎"You're getting the tiles. Kitchen is mine, plus the laundry."
‎Ben thought about it. "Can I do my vacuuming on Saturday? I have training tonight."
‎"You can," Mum said. "But it's a deal, not a suggestion. If you put it off, I move it to Friday and you don't like Friday."
‎"Deal."
‎Two hours later, the flat smelled of lemon. Ben stood in his room, surprised.
‎"I found my other trainer shoe."
‎"Under the dust?" Dad asked.
‎"Under a whole civilisation."
‎Mum stuck the rota on the fridge. "Same time next week. Thirty minutes each, and it never becomes archaeology again."

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):

2.‎Which chores did Ben, Dad and Mum take?
3.‎What did Ben find in his room after cleaning?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Mum found thick... ⁦(dust)⁩ on top of the fridge.
She decided the family needed a... ⁦(deep cleaning)⁩.
Ben said it wasn't fair, but nobody had... ⁦(taken out the rubbish)⁩.
Mum suggested dividing the... ⁦(chores)⁩ and making a rota.
Ben had to use the... ⁦(vacuum cleaner)⁩ and take out the rubbish daily.
Dad had to... ⁦(scrub)⁩ the bathroom tiles.
Mum took the kitchen and the... ⁦(laundry)⁩.
Ben asked to... ⁦(put off)⁩ his cleaning until Saturday.
Two hours later the flat smelled of... ⁦(lemon)⁩.
Mum stuck the... ⁦(rota)⁩ on the fridge for every week.

Тема: № 104 Решение завести питомца и первый день с ним дома ⁦(Adopting a pet and the first week at home)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎to adopt [əˈdɒpt] — взять (животное из приюта)
2.‎shelter [ˈʃeltə] — приют
3.‎puppy [ˈpʌpi] — щенок
4.‎bowl [bəʊl] — миска
5.‎lead [liːd] — поводок
6.‎to bark [bɑːk] — лаять
7.‎to chew [tʃuː] — жевать, грызть
8.‎to be patient [biː ˈpeɪʃnt] — быть терпеливым

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
"He's looking at us," whispered Kate.
‎In the corner of the shelter cage, a small brown puppy was watching them with enormous eyes.
‎"Don't," said her husband, Dan. "We came here to look."
‎"We've been 'looking' for four months."
‎Twenty minutes later they signed the papers, bought a bowl, a lead and a bag of food, and drove home with the puppy on Kate's lap.
‎"What are we calling him?" Dan asked at the traffic lights.
‎"Biscuit."
‎"Why Biscuit?"
‎"Because he's the colour of a biscuit and he'll eat anything."
‎The first night was a disaster. Biscuit barked at 2 AM, then at 3 AM, then at nothing at all.
‎Dan sat on the kitchen floor at four in the morning, holding the puppy. "We've made a terrible mistake."
‎"We haven't," Kate said, wrapping a blanket around him. "He's a baby. He was in a shelter yesterday. Everything smells wrong."
‎By day three, Biscuit had chewed one slipper, half a chair leg and the TV remote.
‎"The remote?" Dan said. "Why the remote?"
‎"Teething," the vet explained on the phone. "Give him something legal to chew and hide your shoes. And keep the same routine — food, walk, sleep, at the same times every day. Dogs relax when they know what's coming."
‎They made a chart. Kate did mornings, Dan did evenings. Walk at seven, food at eight, lights off at ten.
‎On Sunday, Kate came home from the shop and found Biscuit asleep by the door, his nose on Dan's slipper — the surviving one.
‎"He waited for you," Dan said quietly.
‎Kate sat down on the floor and stroked his ears. "Four nights of no sleep. And I'd do it again."
‎"Me too," Dan admitted. "But we're buying a second remote."

Тема: № 105 Визит к ветеринару: прививки и неожиданные новости ⁦(Taking a pet to the vet)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎vet [vet] — ветеринар
2.‎vaccination [ˌvæksɪˈneɪʃn] — вакцинация, прививка
3.‎to examine [ɪɡˈzæmɪn] — осматривать
4.‎symptoms [ˈsɪmptəmz] — симптомы
5.‎to prescribe [prɪˈskraɪb] — выписывать (рецепт, диету)
6.‎diet [ˈdaɪət] — диета, рацион
7.‎to recover [rɪˈkʌvə] — выздоравливать
8.‎check-up [ˈtʃek ʌp] — профилактический осмотр

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
Kate put Biscuit into his carrier and drove to the local vet clinic. It was time for his annual check-up and vaccinations.

"He's been a good boy," Kate told the receptionist. "But he's been scratching his ear a lot lately."

Dr. Evans, a friendly vet with a calm voice, called them into the room. She gently took Biscuit out of the carrier and placed him on the metal table.

"Let's have a look," she said, examining his ears and listening to his heart. "His heart is strong, and his teeth are in great condition. Good job, Kate."

"Thank you," Kate smiled. "What about his ear?"

"Just a mild irritation. I'll prescribe some ear drops. Use them twice a day for a week, and he should recover completely." Dr. Evans then gave him his vaccinations. Biscuit barely flinched.

However, Dr. Evans paused and looked at Biscuit's weight chart. "Now, about his weight. He's gained two kilos since last year. Is he getting many treats?"

Kate blushed. "Well, he's very cute when he begs. Maybe a few extra biscuits."

"I understand," Dr. Evans laughed. "But we need to put him on a strict diet. No more human food. Measure his portions carefully, and increase his daily walks. Obesity can cause serious health issues in dogs."

Kate nodded, feeling a bit guilty but determined. "Okay, no more biscuits. Just healthy dog food and longer walks."

"Perfect. He's a lovely dog, and with a little discipline, he'll be in perfect shape."

On the way home, Biscuit looked at Kate with his big, hopeful eyes. 

"I'm sorry, buddy," Kate said, scratching his head. "No more treats. But we'll go to the park for an extra hour."

Biscuit wagged his tail. He didn't understand the word 'diet', but he definitely understood 'park'.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Why did Kate take Biscuit to the vet?
2.‎What did Dr. Evans prescribe for Biscuit's ear?
3.‎What unexpected news did Kate hear about Biscuit's health?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Kate took Biscuit to the vet for a... ⁦(check-up)⁩ and vaccinations.
Dr. Evans... ⁦(examined)⁩ him and said his heart and teeth were fine.
For his itchy ear, the vet... ⁦(prescribed)⁩ some drops.
However, the vet noticed that Biscuit had gained... ⁦(weight)⁩.
She explained that he needed a strict... ⁦(diet)⁩ and more walks.
Kate felt guilty about giving him too many... ⁦(treats)⁩.
She promised to feed him healthy food and take him to the... ⁦(park)⁩.
Biscuit wagged his tail, happy about the... ⁦(walk)⁩.

Тема: № 106 Помощь ребенку с домашним заданием ⁦(Helping a child with homework)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎homework [ˈhəʊmwɜːk] — домашнее задание
2.‎to struggle [ˈstrʌɡl] — испытывать трудности
3.‎patient [ˈpeɪʃnt] — терпеливый
4.‎to explain [ɪkˈspleɪn] — объяснять
5.‎fraction [ˈfrækʃn] — дробь (в математике)
6.‎to give up [ɡɪv ʌp] — сдаваться
7.‎encouragement [ɪnˈkʌrɪdʒmənt] — поддержка, ободрение
8.‎to understand [ˌʌndəˈstænd] — понимать

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~270 слов.
Mark sat at the kitchen table, staring at a math workbook. His eight-year-old daughter, Lily, was chewing her pencil and looking close to tears.

"I don't get it, Dad," she whispered. "Fractions are stupid."

Mark took a deep breath. He wanted to be patient, but he had been explaining the same problem for twenty minutes. "It's not stupid, Lily. Look, if you have one pizza and you cut it into four pieces..."

"But we don't have a pizza!" Lily argued, her voice rising. "We have numbers! And I don't know what three-quarters means!"

Mark felt his frustration growing. He was an engineer; math was easy for him. Why couldn't she just see it? He almost told her to give up and ask her teacher tomorrow. 

But then he looked at her face. She wasn't being difficult; she was genuinely struggling. 

"Okay," Mark said softly, closing the workbook. "Let's take a break. Do we have any apples in the fridge?"

Lily nodded, confused. 

Mark took an apple and a knife. He cut it into four equal pieces. "Here is one whole apple. Now, I eat one piece. How many pieces are left?"

"Three," Lily said.

"Right. And the whole apple was cut into four pieces. So, what fraction of the apple is left?"

Lily's eyes widened. "Three... quarters?"

"Exactly!" Mark smiled. "Three-quarters. See? It's just pieces of something real."

Lily grabbed the pencil again. "Can we do the next one with a banana?"

Mark laughed. "Sure. We can use anything."

With a little encouragement and real-life examples, Lily finished her homework in ten minutes. Mark realized that teaching wasn't about knowing the answer; it was about finding the right way to explain it.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Why was Lily close to tears at the kitchen table?
2.‎Why did Mark almost tell her to give up?
3.‎How did Mark finally help Lily understand fractions?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Lily was... ⁦(struggling)⁩ with her math homework about fractions.
Mark tried to... ⁦(explain)⁩ it, but she didn't understand.
He felt frustrated and almost told her to... ⁦(give up)⁩.
Instead, he decided to be... ⁦(patient)⁩ and took a break.
He cut an... ⁦(apple)⁩ into four pieces to show her the concept.
Lily finally... ⁦(understood)⁩ that three pieces left meant three-quarters.
With some... ⁦(encouragement)⁩, she finished her homework quickly.
Mark learned that teaching requires finding the right... ⁦(way)⁩ to explain things.

Тема: № 107 Обучение подростка вождению автомобиля ⁦(Teaching a teenager to drive)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎driving lesson [ˈdraɪvɪŋ ˈlesn] — урок вождения
2.‎nervous [ˈnɜːvəs] — нервничающий
3.‎clutch [klʌtʃ] — сцепление (в машине)
4.‎to stall [stɔːl] — глохнуть (о двигателе)
5.‎mirror [ˈmɪrə] — зеркало
6.‎to indicate [ˈɪndɪkeɪt] — включать поворотник
7.‎patience [ˈpeɪʃns] — терпение
8.‎to panic [ˈpænɪk] — паниковать

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
"Okay, Leo, foot on the clutch, slowly release it, and gently press the gas," David said, gripping the door handle tightly.

His sixteen-year-old son, Leo, was sweating. "I'm trying, Dad. But it keeps shaking."

"That means you're finding the bite point. Just a little more..."

Suddenly, the car jerked violently and the engine died. 

"I stalled it again!" Leo groaned, hitting the steering wheel. "I'm terrible at this. Maybe I should just take the bus forever."

David took a deep breath. He remembered his own first driving lesson and how his father had yelled at him. He decided to do better. 

"You're not terrible," David said calmly. "You're learning. Stalling is completely normal. Every driver does it. Just put the car in neutral, turn the key, and try again."

Leo restarted the engine. "What about the mirrors? I keep forgetting to check them."

"Good point. Before you move, check your rearview mirror and your side mirrors. And don't forget to indicate before you turn."

Leo nodded, his hands white on the wheel. He checked the mirrors, signaled, and slowly released the clutch. The car moved forward smoothly. 

"Yes! I did it!" Leo grinned.

"Great job. Now, let's practice stopping. Foot on the brake, clutch down..."

They spent an hour in the empty parking lot. Leo stalled the car three more times, but he didn't panic. He just restarted it and tried again. David's patience made all the difference.

At the end of the lesson, Leo turned off the engine and looked at his dad. "Thanks for not yelling at me."

David smiled. "Driving is a big responsibility. You need to stay calm, and so do I. You did really well today."

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Why did Leo want to give up driving at the beginning?
2.‎How did David react when Leo stalled the car?
3.‎What three things did David remind Leo to do before moving?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Leo was very... ⁦(nervous)⁩ during his driving lesson.
He kept... ⁦(stalling)⁩ the car and felt like he was terrible at it.
David showed... ⁦(patience)⁩ and told him that stalling is normal.
He reminded Leo to check his... ⁦(mirrors)⁩ before moving.
He also told him to... ⁦(indicate)⁩ before turning.
Leo practiced for an hour and didn't... ⁦(panic)⁩ when he made mistakes.
At the end, Leo thanked his dad for not... ⁦(yelling)⁩.
David said that staying... ⁦(calm)⁩ is important for both of them.

Тема: № 108 Забота о пожилых родителях и баланс с работой ⁦(Caring for an elderly parent)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎elderly [ˈeldəli] — пожилой
2.‎to balance [ˈbæləns] — балансировать, совмещать
3.‎appointment [əˈpɔɪntmənt] — запись, прием (у врача)
4.‎to worry [ˈwʌri] — беспокоиться
5.‎support [səˈpɔːt] — поддержка
6.‎independent [ˌɪndɪˈpendənt] — независимый, самостоятельный
7.‎to arrange [əˈreɪndʒ] — организовывать, договариваться
8.‎guilt [ɡɪlt] — чувство вины

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~270 слов.
Sarah stared at her phone. She had a meeting with her boss in ten minutes, but her mother had just called. 

"Sarah, I forgot how to turn on the heating again," her mother's voice sounded weak and confused. "And I think I missed my doctor's appointment."

Sarah felt a familiar wave of guilt. "It's okay, Mum. I'll call the clinic and reschedule. Just sit tight, I'll come over after work."

She hung up and sighed. Balancing her demanding job and caring for her elderly mother was becoming impossible. She was constantly tired and worried.

That evening, she talked to her brother, Tom. "I can't keep doing this, Tom. I'm failing at work, and I'm failing Mum."

Tom listened quietly. "You're not failing, Sarah. You're just one person. Mum needs more support than we can give her while working full-time."

"But she wants to stay independent," Sarah argued. "She hates the idea of a nursing home."

"I know," Tom said gently. "But there's a middle ground. We can arrange for a part-time caregiver. Someone to come in the mornings, help with breakfast, and make sure she takes her medicine. She stays in her own home, but we get peace of mind."

Sarah thought about it. "Do you think she'd agree?"

"Let's talk to her together," Tom suggested. "Frame it as 'extra help', not 'replacement'."

The next day, they visited their mother. To their surprise, she was relieved. "I was so scared of bothering you with every little thing," she admitted. "Having someone to help with the heating and the shopping sounds wonderful."

Sarah finally felt the weight lift off her shoulders. She realized that asking for help wasn't a sign of weakness. It was the only way to truly balance her life and give her mother the care she deserved.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Why did Sarah feel guilty at the beginning of the story?
2.‎What solution did Tom suggest to help their mother?
3.‎How did their mother react to the idea of a caregiver?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Sarah was struggling to... ⁦(balance)⁩ her job and caring for her elderly mother.
Her mother called because she forgot how to use the... ⁦(heating)⁩ and missed an appointment.
Sarah felt a lot of... ⁦(guilt)⁩ and worry.
Her brother Tom suggested they... ⁦(arrange)⁩ for a part-time caregiver.
This way, their mother could stay... ⁦(independent)⁩ in her own home.
They talked to her, and she was actually... ⁦(relieved)⁩.
She didn't want to be a... ⁦(bother)⁩ to her children.
Sarah realized that asking for... ⁦(help)⁩ was the right decision.

Тема: № 109 Организация семейного праздника или воссоединения ⁦(Planning a family reunion or holiday gathering)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎reunion [ˌriːˈjuːniən] — воссоединение, встреча
2.‎to organize [ˈɔːɡənaɪz] — организовывать
3.‎guest list [ɡest lɪst] — список гостей
4.‎dietary requirements [ˈdaɪətəri rɪˈkwaɪəmənts] — диетические требования
5.‎to delegate [ˈdelɪɡeɪt] — делегировать, поручать
6.‎venue [ˈvenjuː] — место проведения
7.‎chaos [ˈkeɪɒs] — хаос
8.‎memorable [ˈmemərəbl] — незабываемый

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
Emma looked at the whiteboard in her kitchen. It was covered in notes, and she was rubbing her temples. The family reunion was in two weeks, and she was organizing it all by herself.

"Forty people," she muttered. "Forty people, three vegetarians, one gluten-free, and Uncle Bob who is allergic to peanuts. This is going to be chaos."

Her husband, Mark, walked in with two cups of coffee. "You look like you're planning a military operation, not a family picnic."

"It feels like one," Emma sighed. "I'm trying to finalize the guest list, book the venue, and figure out the catering. I can't do it all."

"Then don't," Mark said simply. "Delegate."

"Delegate to who? Everyone is busy."

"Your cousins love to help. Call Sarah and ask her to handle the dietary requirements. Ask Mike to book the park venue and organize the games. You just manage the main guest list and the music."

Emma hesitated. "But what if they mess it up?"

"Then it's a family reunion, not a Michelin-star restaurant. It will be fine."

Emma took a deep breath and picked up her phone. She called Sarah, who was thrilled to handle the food. She texted Mike, who immediately found a great park with a barbecue area. 

Within two days, the whiteboard was much less scary. Emma only had to confirm the final numbers and make a playlist.

On the day of the reunion, the sun was shining. Sarah had prepared beautiful salads, Mike had set up a fantastic volleyball net, and the music was playing. 

Emma stood by the barbecue, watching her family laugh and talk. Mark handed her a drink. "See? Not chaos."

"No," Emma smiled, clinking her glass against his. "It's going to be memorable."

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Why was Emma stressed about the family reunion?
2.‎What advice did Mark give her?
3.‎What tasks did Sarah and Mike take on?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Emma was trying to... ⁦(organize)⁩ a family reunion for forty people.
She was worried about the... ⁦(guest list)⁩ and special food needs.
Mark told her not to do it all and to... ⁦(delegate)⁩ tasks.
Emma asked Sarah to handle the... ⁦(dietary requirements)⁩.
She asked Mike to book the... ⁦(venue)⁩ and organize games.
Emma realized it wouldn't be... ⁦(chaos)⁩ if she shared the work.
On the day of the event, everything was perfect.
Emma knew the day would be truly... ⁦(memorable)⁩.

Тема: № 110 Плановый визит к стоматологу или внезапная зубная боль ⁦(Going to the dentist for a check-up or emergency)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎dentist [ˈdentɪst] — стоматолог
2.‎toothache [ˈtuːθeɪk] — зубная боль
3.‎appointment [əˈpɔɪntmənt] — запись, прием
4.‎filling [ˈfɪlɪŋ] — пломба
5.‎to drill [drɪl] — сверлить
6.‎cavity [ˈkævəti] — кариес, полость в зубе
7.‎to rinse [rɪns] — полоскать
8.‎painkiller [ˈpeɪnkɪlə] — обезболивающее

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~270 слов.
Tom woke up at 6 AM with a sharp pain in his lower jaw. He touched his cheek. It was swollen. 

"Oh no," he groaned. "Not today."

He had an important presentation at work, but the toothache was unbearable. He took a painkiller, but it only took the edge off. By 9 AM, he knew he had to call the dentist.

"Hello, Bright Smile Dental," the receptionist answered.

"Hi, I'm Tom Reed. I have a severe toothache and I need an emergency appointment."

"Let me check... Dr. Miller has a cancellation at 11:30. Can you make it?"

"Yes, absolutely. Thank you."

At the clinic, Dr. Miller examined Tom's mouth and took an X-ray. 

"Well, Tom, you have a small cavity in your back molar," she explained. "It's reached the nerve, which is why it hurts so much. We need to clean it and put in a filling."

Tom gripped the chair. "Will it hurt?"

"I'll give you a local injection, so you won't feel a thing. You'll just hear the drill."

The procedure took twenty minutes. Tom was right; he felt no pain, just a strange vibration. When it was over, Dr. Miller handed him a small cup. "Rinse your mouth, please."

"All done," she smiled. "The filling is set. Avoid very hot or cold food for a day, and please, floss more often!"

Tom left the clinic feeling incredibly relieved. The pain was gone. He made it to his office just in time for his presentation, and for the first time in his life, he bought dental floss on his way home.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Why did Tom have to call the dentist in the morning?
2.‎What did the X-ray show about Tom's tooth?
3.‎What advice did Dr. Miller give Tom after the procedure?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:

He took a... ⁦(painkiller)⁩, but it didn't help much.
He called the clinic and got an emergency... ⁦(appointment)⁩ at 11:30.
The dentist found a small... ⁦(cavity)⁩ that reached the nerve.
She gave him an injection and used a... ⁦(drill)⁩ to clean the tooth.
Then she put in a... ⁦(filling)⁩.
Tom had to... ⁦(rinse)⁩ his mouth and avoid hot food for a day.
He left the clinic pain-free and finally bought some... ⁦(floss)⁩.

Тема: № 111 Разговор со страховой компанией о покрытии расходов ⁦(Navigating health insurance and doctor visits)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎insurance [ɪnˈʃʊərəns] — страхование
2.‎coverage [ˈkʌvərɪdʒ] — покрытие, страховое покрытие
3.‎claim [kleɪm] — требование выплаты, страховой случай
4.‎policy [ˈpɒləsi] — страховой полис
5.‎premium [ˈpriːmiəm] — страховой взнос
6.‎to cover [ˈkʌvə] — покрывать (расходы)
7.‎deductible [dɪˈdʌktəbl] — франшиза, сумма, оплачиваемая самостоятельно
8.‎representative [ˌreprɪˈzentətɪv] — представитель

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
Lisa held the phone to her ear, listening to the classical music. She had been on hold for ten minutes, trying to understand her health insurance bill.

Finally, a voice answered. "Thank you for calling SafeHealth Insurance. My name is David. How can I help you?"

"Hi, David. I'm calling about my policy. I visited a specialist last week, and I just got a bill for $150. I thought my insurance covered specialist visits."

"Let me pull up your account," David said. "Okay, Lisa, I see your policy. You have a $500 deductible. This means you have to pay the first $500 of your medical expenses out of pocket each year before our coverage kicks in."

Lisa sighed. "Oh, I didn't realize that. So this $150 counts toward the deductible?"

"Exactly," David explained. "Once you reach $500, the insurance will cover 80% of your specialist visits. You just need to file a claim for this visit so we can track your progress."

"How do I file a claim?" 

"It's very easy. You can do it on our website. Just upload a photo of the doctor's invoice and your receipt. The system will process it automatically."

"That sounds simple enough," Lisa said, feeling much less stressed. "One more thing: does my premium go up if I file a claim?"

"No, health insurance premiums are fixed for the year. Filing a claim for necessary medical care won't change your monthly payment."

"Thank you, David. You've been very helpful."

"You're welcome, Lisa. Have a great day."

Lisa hung up and immediately opened her laptop. She uploaded the receipt, filed the claim, and made a note in her calendar: only $350 left until full coverage. It wasn't free, but at least she finally understood how it worked.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Why did Lisa call her insurance company?
2.‎What is a "deductible" according to the representative?
3.‎How can Lisa file a claim?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Lisa called her... ⁦(insurance)⁩ company because she got an unexpected bill.
The... ⁦(representative)⁩ explained that she had a $500... ⁦(deductible)⁩.
This means she has to pay the first expenses... ⁦(herself)⁩.
The $150 bill will count toward this... ⁦(limit)⁩.
After that, the insurance will... ⁦(cover)⁩ 80% of the costs.
Lisa learned to file a... ⁦(claim)⁩ by uploading a photo of the receipt online.
She also learned that her monthly... ⁦(premium)⁩ will not go up.
Finally, she understood how her... ⁦(policy)⁩ worked.

Тема: № 112 Первый поход в спортзал и знакомство с тренером ⁦(Starting a new fitness routine or joining a gym)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎gym [dʒɪm] — спортзал
2.‎trainer [ˈtreɪnə] — тренер
3.‎routine [ruːˈtiːn] — режим, программа тренировок
4.‎to lift weights [lɪft weɪts] — поднимать тяжести
5.‎equipment [ɪˈkwɪpmənt] — оборудование, тренажеры
6.‎to stretch [stretʃ] — растягиваться
7.‎sore [sɔː] — болезненный, ноющий (о мышцах)
8.‎to motivate [ˈməʊtɪveɪt] — мотивировать

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
Alex stood outside the 'FitLife' gym, feeling completely intimidated. Through the glass windows, he could see people lifting heavy weights and running fast on treadmills. 

"I can't do this," he muttered, turning to leave.

"First day?" a friendly voice asked. 

Alex turned around. A tall man in a gym uniform was smiling at him. "I'm Mike, one of the trainers here. You look like you're about to run away."

"Is it that obvious?" Alex laughed nervously. "I haven't exercised in five years. I don't even know how to use the equipment."

"That's exactly why we're here," Mike said. "Come inside. I'll give you a free orientation. No heavy lifting today, I promise."

Inside, the gym was loud, but Mike led Alex to a quiet corner. "Let's start with the basics. First rule of the gym: always stretch before and after your workout. It prevents injuries and makes you less sore tomorrow."

Mike showed Alex some simple stretches. Then, he introduced him to the machines. "Don't worry about the free weights yet. Let's use the resistance machines. They guide your movement and are much safer for beginners."

For the next thirty minutes, Mike showed Alex how to adjust the seats, set the weight, and breathe properly. He didn't push Alex to do too much. 

"How do you feel?" Mike asked at the end.

"Actually, pretty good," Alex admitted. "I thought I would be laughed at, but you made it seem easy."

"Everyone starts somewhere," Mike smiled. "I can write a simple beginner routine for you. Three days a week, thirty minutes. Deal?"

"Deal," Alex said, feeling a new spark of motivation. He realized that the gym wasn't a scary place for superhumans. It was just a place to start getting healthier, one small step at a time.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):

2.‎What was the first rule of the gym that Mike taught Alex?
3.‎Why did Mike suggest using resistance machines instead of free weights?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:

A friendly... ⁦(trainer)⁩ named Mike stopped him from leaving.
Mike offered a free orientation and showed him the... ⁦(equipment)⁩.
He taught Alex to always... ⁦(stretch)⁩ to avoid getting sore.
They used resistance machines because they are safer for... ⁦(beginners)⁩.
Mike didn't push him too hard and made him feel... ⁦(comfortable)⁩.
He offered to write a simple beginner... ⁦(routine)⁩ for three days a week.
Alex left feeling... ⁦(motivated)⁩ to start his fitness journey.

Тема: № 113 Легкая простуда или травма во время отпуска ⁦(Dealing with a minor illness or injury while traveling)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎pharmacy [ˈfɑːməsi] — аптека
2.‎to twist [twɪst] — подвернуть (сустав)
3.‎ankle [ˈæŋkl] — лодыжка, щиколотка
4.‎fever [ˈfiːvə] — температура, лихорадка
5.‎prescription [prɪˈskrɪpʃn] — рецепт
6.‎over-the-counter [ˌəʊvə ðə ˈkaʊntə] — безрецептурный
7.‎to rest [rest] — отдыхать
8.‎travel insurance [ˈtrævl ɪnˈʃʊərəns] — туристическая страховка

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
"Ouch!" Sarah cried out, stumbling on the uneven cobblestone street in Rome. 

"Are you okay?" her husband, Mark, asked, rushing to her side.

"I think I twisted my ankle," Sarah said, wincing as she put weight on her left foot. "It’s swelling up."

They limped to a nearby bench. Sarah’s ankle was already turning red. "We have a museum tour in an hour," she said, disappointed.

"Forget the tour," Mark replied firmly. "Let’s find a pharmacy."

They found a green cross sign on the corner. Inside, a friendly pharmacist examined Sarah’s ankle. "It’s a mild sprain," he said in English. "No fracture. You need to rest it, keep it elevated, and apply ice."

"Do I need a prescription for painkillers?" Mark asked.

"No, I can give you an over-the-counter anti-inflammatory gel and some ibuprofen," the pharmacist said, handing them a small bag. "But if the fever develops or the pain gets worse, you must see a doctor."

Back at their Airbnb, Sarah sat on the sofa with her foot propped up on pillows. Mark brought her ice and the gel. 

"Well, this wasn't in the itinerary," Sarah sighed.

"Don't worry," Mark smiled. "We have travel insurance. If we need a doctor, it’s covered. For now, your only job is to rest."

For the next two days, Sarah stayed in, reading books and watching Italian movies. By the third day, the swelling was gone, and she could walk normally. 

"Ready for the Colosseum?" Mark asked on Thursday morning.

"Absolutely," Sarah grinned, grabbing her walking shoes. "But this time, I’m watching my step."

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)

2.‎What did the pharmacist recommend for her ankle?
3.‎How did Sarah spend the next two days?

Mark reminded her that they had... ⁦(travel insurance)⁩ just in case.
Sarah stayed at the Airbnb with her foot... ⁦(elevated)⁩ on pillows.

Тема: № 114 Сдача машины в автосервис и неожиданный счет ⁦(Taking a car to the mechanic for unexpected repairs)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎mechanic [məˈkænɪk] — механик, автослесарь
2.‎breakdown [ˈbreɪkdaʊn] — поломка
3.‎to inspect [ɪnˈspekt] — осматривать, проверять
4.‎estimate [ˈestɪmət] — предварительная оценка стоимости
5.‎brake pads [breɪk pædz] — тормозные колодки
6.‎unexpected [ˌʌnɪkˈspektɪd] — неожиданный
7.‎to approve [əˈpruːv] — одобрять, соглашаться
8.‎invoice [ˈɪnvɔɪs] — счет на оплату

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
"My car is making a weird squeaking noise when I brake," Tom told the receptionist at the auto shop. "I think it just needs an oil change."

The mechanic, a greasy-handed man named Joe, nodded. "Leave it with us. We’ll inspect it and give you a call."

Two hours later, Tom’s phone rang. 

"Hi Tom, it’s Joe from the garage. We checked the car. The oil is fine, but we found an unexpected issue."

Tom’s stomach tightened. "Oh no. What is it?"

"Your front brake pads are completely worn out, and the rotor is scratched. If you keep driving, it’s dangerous. We also noticed a small leak in the coolant hose."

"How much will that cost?" Tom asked nervously.

"The estimate for the brake pads and the hose is about $450, including labor," Joe replied. "We won’t do any work without your approval."

Tom did some quick mental math. It was more than he budgeted, but safety came first. "Okay, I approve the repair. How long will it take?"

"We can have it ready by 5 PM today."

When Tom returned in the evening, Joe handed him the keys and the final invoice. "All done. We also gave the car a quick wash."

Tom looked at the invoice. It was exactly $450. "Thanks, Joe. I’m glad you caught that before a real breakdown."

"Anytime," Joe smiled. "Better to fix it now than to pay for a tow truck later."

Tom drove home smoothly. The squeaking was gone, and despite the unexpected bill, he felt relieved knowing his car was safe for the upcoming road trip.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Why did Tom take his car to the auto shop initially?
2.‎What unexpected issues did the mechanic find?
3.‎How much did the final repair cost?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Tom thought his car just needed an oil... ⁦(change)⁩ because of a squeaking noise.
The mechanic decided to... ⁦(inspect)⁩ the car thoroughly.
He found that the... ⁦(brake pads)⁩ were worn out and there was a leak.
Joe gave Tom an... ⁦(estimate)⁩ of $450 for the repairs.
Tom decided to... ⁦(approve)⁩ the work because safety is important.
The mechanic promised to finish the job by 5... ⁦(PM)⁩.
When Tom returned, he paid the final... ⁦(invoice)⁩.
He was relieved that the car was safe and the squeaking was... ⁦(gone)⁩.

Тема: № 115 Открытие банковского счета или разблокировка карты ⁦(Opening a bank account or dealing with a frozen card)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎bank account [bæŋk əˈkaʊnt] — банковский счет
2.‎to freeze [friːz] — замораживать, блокировать
3.‎PIN [pɪn] — ПИН-код
4.‎identity [aɪˈdentəti] — личность, удостоверение личности
5.‎branch [brɑːntʃ] — отделение (банка)
6.‎customer service [ˈkʌstəmə ˈsɜːvɪs] — служба поддержки клиентов
7.‎to verify [ˈverɪfaɪ] — подтверждать, проверять
8.‎transaction [trænˈzækʃn] — транзакция, операция

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
Lisa stood at the grocery store checkout, swiping her card. 

"Declined," the cashier said flatly. 

Lisa frowned and tried again. "Declined."

"That’s embarrassing," she muttered, putting the card away and paying with cash. 

At home, she immediately called the bank’s customer service number on the back of her card. After a short wait, a representative answered.

"Hello, this is David from City Bank. How can I help you?"

"Hi, David. My card was just declined at the supermarket. Is there a problem with my bank account?"

"Let me check," David said. "Ah, I see the issue. Our security system flagged an unusual transaction and automatically decided to freeze your card to protect your money."

"An unusual transaction? I haven't bought anything strange," Lisa said, confused.

"Sometimes the system is overly cautious," David explained. "To verify your identity and unfreeze the card, I just need to ask you two security questions. What is your mother’s maiden name and the last four digits of your social security number?"

Lisa answered them correctly. 

"Perfect. Your identity is verified. I have unfrozen your card. It will work immediately. I also recommend setting up transaction alerts on your phone so you know what’s happening."

"Thank you so much, David. That was quick and easy."

"You’re welcome, Lisa. Have a great day."

Lisa hung up, feeling relieved. She went back to the store, bought her groceries without any issues, and immediately downloaded the bank’s mobile app to set up those alerts.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎What happened when Lisa tried to pay at the grocery store?
2.‎Why did the bank freeze her card?
3.‎How did Lisa verify her identity to the representative?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:

To fix this, David needed to... ⁦(verify)⁩ her identity.
Lisa answered two security questions correctly.
David then... ⁦(unfroze)⁩ the card immediately.
He advised her to set up... ⁦(alerts)⁩ on her phone.
Lisa went back to the store and paid without any... ⁦(problems)⁩.

Тема: № 116 Возврат бракованного товара в магазин ⁦(Returning a defective item to a store)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎receipt [rɪˈsiːt] — чек
2.‎defective [dɪˈfektɪv] — бракованный, неисправный
3.‎refund [ˈriːfʌnd] — возврат денег
4.‎exchange [ɪksˈtʃeɪndʒ] — обмен
5.‎warranty [ˈwɒrənti] — гарантия
6.‎customer service desk [ˈkʌstəmə ˈsɜːvɪs desk] — стойка обслуживания клиентов
7.‎to process [ˈprəʊses] — оформлять (возврат)
8.‎faulty [ˈfɔːlti] — неисправный

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~270 слов.
Mark put on his new wireless headphones and pressed play. The left earpiece was completely silent. 

"Great," he sighed. "A faulty product."

He had bought them just three days ago. He found the box, the charging cable, and most importantly, the paper receipt. He put everything back in the bag and drove to the electronics store.

At the customer service desk, a young woman named Chloe smiled. "Hi there, how can I help you today?"

"Hi, I bought these headphones on Tuesday, but the left side isn't working. It’s defective," Mark explained, placing the box and the receipt on the counter.

Chloe inspected the headphones and scanned the receipt. "I’m sorry to hear that. Since it’s been less than fourteen days, you have two options: a full refund or an exchange for a new pair."

"I’d prefer an exchange, please. I really like this model," Mark said.

"Sure thing," Chloe replied. "Let me just process the return in the system." She typed on her computer for a moment. "The warranty covers this, so there’s no restocking fee. Here is your brand new, sealed box."

She handed him a fresh package. "Would you like to open it and test it right here to be sure?"

Mark opened the box, plugged in the charging cable, and checked the connection on his phone. Both sides worked perfectly. 

"Perfect. Thank you for your help, Chloe."

"No problem at all. Have a great day!"

Mark walked out of the store feeling satisfied. He realized that having the receipt and knowing the store’s return policy made the whole experience quick and stress-free.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎What was wrong with the headphones Mark bought?
2.‎What two options did the store offer him?
3.‎Why was the return process quick and easy?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Mark discovered that his new headphones were... ⁦(defective)⁩.
He gathered the box, cable, and the... ⁦(receipt)⁩.
He went to the... ⁦(customer service desk)⁩ at the electronics store.
The employee offered him a full... ⁦(refund)⁩ or an exchange.
Mark chose an... ⁦(exchange)⁩ because he liked the model.
The employee checked the... ⁦(warranty)⁩ and processed the return.
Mark tested the new pair in the store to make sure it wasn't... ⁦(faulty)⁩.
He left the store feeling... ⁦(satisfied)⁩ with the service.

Тема: № 117 Жалоба на плохое обслуживание в ресторане или отеле ⁦(Complaining about poor service at a restaurant or hotel)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎complaint [kəmˈpleɪnt] — жалоба
2.‎manager [ˈmænɪdʒə] — менеджер, управляющий
3.‎cold [kəʊld] — холодный
4.‎to apologize [əˈpɒlədʒaɪz] — извиняться
5.‎compensation [ˌkɒmpenˈseɪʃn] — компенсация
6.‎unacceptable [ˌʌnəkˈseptəbl] — неприемлемый
7.‎to resolve [rɪˈzɒlv] — решать, улаживать
8.‎feedback [ˈfiːdbæk] — обратная связь

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
Emma and her boyfriend, Leo, had been waiting for their food for forty-five minutes. When the waiter finally brought the pasta, it was barely warm. 

Leo took one bite and put his fork down. "This is cold. And we’ve been waiting forever."

Emma nodded. "I’m going to speak to the manager. This is unacceptable for a restaurant of this price."

She waved at the waiter. "Could you please bring the manager to our table?"

A few moments later, a man in a neat suit approached. "Good evening. I’m the manager, David. I understand you have a complaint?"

"Yes," Emma said calmly but firmly. "We ordered our food forty-five minutes ago, and the pasta is cold. We are very disappointed."

David looked at the plate and frowned. "You are absolutely right. This is not our standard. I sincerely apologize for the long wait and the temperature of the food. It’s completely unacceptable."

He turned to the waiter. "Please take this back to the kitchen. Tell the chef to prepare two fresh portions immediately, on the house."

He looked back at Emma. "As compensation for the delay, your entire meal tonight will be free, and I’d like to offer you a complimentary dessert."

Emma’s expression softened. "Thank you, David. We appreciate you resolving this so quickly."

"Of course. We value your feedback, and I hope the rest of your evening is much better."

Ten minutes later, fresh, hot pasta arrived, followed by a delicious chocolate cake. 

"Well," Leo smiled, taking a bite of the cake. "That’s how you handle a complaint."

Emma agreed. The manager’s polite attitude and quick action turned a frustrating experience into a surprisingly good dinner.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Why did Emma decide to speak to the manager?
2.‎How did the manager react to their complaint?
3.‎What compensation did the restaurant offer?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Emma and Leo waited forty-five minutes for their... ⁦(food)⁩.
When it arrived, the pasta was... ⁦(cold)⁩.
Emma calmly asked to speak to the... ⁦(manager)⁩.
The manager listened and decided to... ⁦(apologize)⁩ sincerely.
He said the service was... ⁦(unacceptable)⁩ and took the plates away.
He offered a complimentary meal and dessert as... ⁦(compensation)⁩.
Emma appreciated that he managed to... ⁦(resolve)⁩ the issue quickly.
The fresh food arrived, and they left positive... ⁦(feedback)⁩.

Тема: № 118 Покупка подержанного автомобиля и торг ⁦(Buying a used car and negotiating the price)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎second-hand [ˌsekənd ˈhænd] — подержанный, б/у
2.‎test drive [test draɪv] — пробная поездка
3.‎mileage [ˈmaɪlɪdʒ] — пробег
4.‎negotiation [nɪˌɡəʊʃɪˈeɪʃn] — переговоры, торг
5.‎scratch [skrætʃ] — царапина
6.‎to lower the price [ˈləʊə ðə praɪs] — снизить цену
7.‎deal [diːl] — сделка, договоренность
8.‎mechanic's inspection [məˈkænɪks ɪnˈspekʃn] — осмотр механика

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
Alex stood in front of a blue Honda Civic. The online ad said it was in perfect condition, but Alex noticed a long scratch on the rear door.

"Nice car, right?" the seller, a guy named Greg, said, walking up. 

"It’s okay," Alex replied. "But the ad didn’t mention this scratch. And the mileage is a bit higher than I expected."

Greg shrugged. "It’s just a small scratch. The engine runs perfectly. Want to take it for a test drive?"

"Sure," Alex said. 

The drive was smooth, but Alex could hear a faint rattling noise when he accelerated. 

Back at the parking lot, Alex crossed his arms. "I like the car, Greg, but it needs some work. The scratch needs buffing, and there’s a rattling sound. I was thinking of offering $4,000."

Greg frowned. "The asking price is $5,500. $4,000 is too low."

"I understand," Alex said calmly. "But I will need to pay for a mechanic's inspection before I buy it. If there are hidden issues, it will cost me more. How about $4,800, and I take it as is?"

Greg thought for a moment, rubbing his chin. "Make it $5,000, and I’ll throw in a full tank of gas."

Alex smiled and extended his hand. "Deal."

They shook hands. Alex took the car to his trusted mechanic the next day. The inspection revealed only minor issues, nothing major. 

Alex drove his new second-hand car home, feeling proud. He realized that a little bit of observation and calm negotiation can save you a lot of money.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎What two problems did Alex notice about the car initially?
2.‎What was the seller's initial asking price?
3.‎What did Alex insist on doing before finalizing the purchase?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Alex looked at a... ⁦(second-hand)⁩ blue Honda Civic.
He noticed a... ⁦(scratch)⁩ on the door and high mileage.
He took the car for a... ⁦(test drive)⁩ and heard a rattling noise.
During the... ⁦(negotiation)⁩, Alex offered $4,000.
The seller wanted $5,500, but they finally agreed on... ⁦($5,000)⁩.
Alex made a... ⁦(deal)⁩ and got a full tank of gas.
Before driving it home, he took it for a... ⁦(mechanic's inspection)⁩.
He learned that calm negotiation helps to... ⁦(lower the price)⁩.

Тема: № 119 Столкновение с телефонным мошенником или фишингом ⁦(Dealing with a scam call or phishing email)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎scam [skæm] — мошенничество, афера
2.‎phishing [ˈfɪʃɪŋ] — фишинг (мошенничество через интернет)
3.‎suspicious [səˈspɪʃəs] — подозрительный
4.‎to hang up [hæŋ ʌp] — повесить трубку
5.‎personal details [ˈpɜːsənl ˈdiːteɪlz] — личные данные
6.‎to block [blɒk] — блокировать
7.‎fraud [frɔːd] — мошенничество, обман
8.‎legitimate [lɪˈdʒɪtɪmət] — законный, подлинный

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~270 слов.
Sarah’s phone rang while she was making dinner. The caller ID showed "Bank Security". 

"Hello?" she answered.

"Good afternoon, this is John from your bank’s fraud department," a serious voice said. "We have detected suspicious activity on your account. To secure your funds, I need you to confirm your PIN and your mother’s maiden name."

Sarah paused. Something felt wrong. A legitimate bank would never ask for a PIN over the phone. 

"Can you give me your employee ID number, please?" Sarah asked calmly.

There was a brief silence on the other end. "Ma'am, this is an urgent security matter. We need those details now."

"I’m not giving you any personal details," Sarah said firmly. "I will call the bank myself using the number on the back of my card."

"Fine," the voice snapped, and the line went dead. He had decided to hang up.

Sarah immediately called her actual bank. The real representative checked her account. "Your account is perfectly safe, Ms. Davis. No unusual activity. You just avoided a scam call."

"Thank goodness," Sarah sighed. "He tried to get my PIN."

"Please never share that information," the representative advised. "We also recommend blocking that number and reporting the fraud attempt through our app."

Sarah did exactly that. She blocked the number, reported the incident, and made a mental note: never trust urgent calls asking for passwords. 

Later that evening, she received a phishing email with a similar message, asking her to "click here to verify her account". She smiled, deleted it, and moved it to the spam folder. She was ready for them.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎What did the caller ask Sarah to confirm?
2.‎Why did Sarah realize the call was a scam?
3.‎What did Sarah do after hanging up the phone?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Sarah received a call from someone claiming to be from... ⁦(Bank Security)⁩.
The caller asked for her PIN and other... ⁦(personal details)⁩.
Sarah felt it was... ⁦(suspicious)⁩ because a real bank never asks for a PIN.
She asked for his employee ID, and the caller decided to... ⁦(hang up)⁩.
Sarah called her real bank and confirmed her account was... ⁦(safe)⁩.
She decided to... ⁦(block)⁩ the scammer's number.
She also reported the... ⁦(fraud)⁩ attempt to the bank.
Later, she deleted a similar... ⁦(phishing)⁩ email without clicking anything.

Тема: № 120 Онлайн-шоппинг: получение не того товара и общение с поддержкой ⁦(Online shopping: receiving the wrong item and contacting support)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎order [ˈɔːdə] — заказ
2.‎delivery [dɪˈlɪvəri] — доставка
3.‎wrong size [rɒŋ saɪz] — неправильный размер
4.‎customer support [ˈkʌstəmə səˈpɔːt] — служба поддержки
5.‎return label [rɪˈtɜːn ˈleɪbl] — этикетка для возврата
6.‎replacement [rɪˈpleɪsmənt] — замена
7.‎tracking number [ˈtrækɪŋ ˈnʌmbə] — номер для отслеживания
8.‎to apologize [əˈpɒlədʒaɪz] — извиняться

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
The delivery box arrived on Tuesday. Emma was excited; she had ordered a blue winter jacket, size medium, for her upcoming trip. 

She tore open the cardboard and pulled out the garment. It was bright red. And it was clearly an extra-large.

"Oh, come on!" Emma groaned. "I specifically ordered a blue medium."

She checked the shipping label. It had her name and address, but the item description was wrong. She took photos of the red jacket and the label, then opened the online store’s chat window to contact customer support.

"Hello! How can I help you today?" the chat agent, Alex, replied instantly.

"Hi, Alex. I received the wrong item in my delivery. I ordered a blue jacket, size M, but I got a red one, size XL."

"I am so sorry for this mistake," Alex replied quickly. "Let me check your order number. Thank you. I see the error in our warehouse. We will send you a replacement immediately, free of charge."

"That’s great. What do I do with this red jacket?" Emma asked.

"I will email you a prepaid return label. Just print it, stick it on the box, and drop it off at any post office. You don’t need to pay for the return shipping."

"Perfect. When will the new jacket arrive?"

"Your replacement will be shipped today. You will receive a tracking number in your email within two hours."

"Thank you, Alex. You made this very easy."

"You’re welcome, Emma. We apologize for the inconvenience. Have a great day!"

Emma printed the label, packed the red jacket, and felt relieved. The mistake was annoying, but the customer support team fixed it so quickly that she didn't even lose a day of her shopping plans.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎What was wrong with the item Emma received?
2.‎How did Emma contact the online store?
3.‎What solution did the customer support agent offer?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Emma received a... ⁦(delivery)⁩ box with a winter jacket.
She opened it and saw it was the wrong color and... ⁦(wrong size)⁩.
She took photos and contacted... ⁦(customer support)⁩ via chat.
The agent checked her... ⁦(order)⁩ and apologized for the warehouse mistake.
They promised to send a free... ⁦(replacement)⁩ immediately.
Emma received a prepaid... ⁦(return label)⁩ to send the red jacket back.
The agent also provided a... ⁦(tracking number)⁩ for the new package.
Emma was happy because the support team managed to... ⁦(resolve)⁩ the issue quickly.

Тема: № 121 Потеря багажа в аэропорту и общение со стойкой регистрации ⁦(Dealing with lost luggage at the airport)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎baggage claim [ˈbæɡɪdʒ kleɪm] — зона выдачи багажа
2.‎lost property [lɒst ˈprɒpəti] — бюро находок
3.‎tracking number [ˈtrækɪŋ ˈnʌmbə] — номер для отслеживания
4.‎essential [ɪˈsenʃl] — необходимый, самый важный
5.‎to compensate [ˈkɒmpenseɪt] — компенсировать
6.‎delayed [dɪˈleɪd] — задержанный
7.‎claim form [kleɪm fɔːm] — бланк заявления (о потере)
8.‎reimbursement [ˌriːɪmˈbɜːsmənt] — возмещение расходов

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
Anna stood in the baggage claim area in Paris, watching the carousel spin. Her flight had landed an hour ago, but her blue suitcase was nowhere to be seen. 

"It’s not here," she said to her friend, Mark, feeling a knot in her stomach. "My clothes, my laptop, everything is in that bag."

"Let’s go to the lost property desk," Mark suggested calmly. "Don’t panic. Airlines lose bags all the time, and they usually find them."

They walked to the airline’s service counter. The agent, a polite woman named Sophie, typed on her keyboard. 

"I’m sorry for the inconvenience," Sophie said. "Your luggage was delayed. It’s still in London. But we have a tracking number for it."

"When will it arrive?" Anna asked, worried. 

"Most likely tomorrow morning. We will deliver it directly to your hotel," Sophie explained. "In the meantime, I will give you a claim form. You can buy essential items like toiletries and a change of clothes, and the airline will compensate you. Keep all your receipts for reimbursement."

Anna filled out the form, providing her hotel address and phone number. Sophie handed her a small kit with a toothbrush and some basic toiletries. 

"Thank you, Sophie. I hope it arrives tomorrow," Anna said, feeling slightly better.

"Me too," Sophie smiled. "You can check the tracking number on our website anytime."

The next day at 10 AM, the hotel receptionist called Anna’s room. "Your suitcase has arrived." 

Anna rushed downstairs. Seeing her blue bag, she let out a huge sigh of relief. The delay was stressful, but the airline’s clear process made it manageable.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Where was Anna when she realized her suitcase was missing?
2.‎What did the airline agent offer to help Anna while she waited?
3.‎When and how did Anna finally get her luggage back?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Anna waited at the... ⁦(baggage claim)⁩, but her suitcase didn't appear.
She and Mark went to the... ⁦(lost property)⁩ desk.
The agent explained that the luggage was... ⁦(delayed)⁩ in London.
She gave Anna a... ⁦(tracking number)⁩ and a claim form.
Anna was told to keep receipts for... ⁦(reimbursement)⁩ of essential items.
The airline promised to deliver the bag to her... ⁦(hotel)⁩.
The next morning, the receptionist called to say the suitcase had... ⁦(arrived)⁩.
Anna was relieved that the process was... ⁦(manageable)⁩.

Тема: № 122 Дорожное путешествие с друзьями: планирование и форс-мажоры ⁦(A road trip with friends: planning and unexpected detours)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎road trip [rəʊd trɪp] — автомобильное путешествие
2.‎detour [ˈdiːtʊə] — объезд, отклонение от маршрута
3.‎flat tyre [flæt ˈtaɪə] — проколотая шина
4.‎to navigate [ˈnævɪɡeɪt] — ориентироваться, прокладывать маршрут
5.‎snacks [snæks] — закуски, снеки
6.‎breakdown [ˈbreɪkdaʊn] — поломка
7.‎to argue [ˈɑːɡjuː] — спорить
8.‎to resolve [rɪˈzɒlv] — решать, улаживать

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
"Are we there yet?" Leo asked from the back seat, eating his third bag of chips.

"Leo, we’ve been driving for only two hours," Sarah replied, gripping the steering wheel. "We still have three hours to the coast."

Their road trip was supposed to be perfect. They had planned the route, packed plenty of snacks, and made a great playlist. But then, the GPS signal suddenly disappeared.

"I think we should have taken the highway," Leo said. 

"I’m navigating fine," Sarah insisted, turning onto a narrow dirt road. "This is a shortcut."

Ten minutes later, there was a loud bang. The car pulled to the side. 

"Great," Sarah sighed, looking out the window. "We have a flat tyre."

Leo groaned. "I told you! Now we’re going to have a breakdown in the middle of nowhere."

"Stop arguing, both of you," said Mike, who was sitting in the front passenger seat. "Panicking won’t fix the tyre. Let’s resolve this together."

Mike grabbed the toolbox from the trunk. "Leo, hold the flashlight. Sarah, help me loosen these bolts."

Working together, they managed to change the tyre in twenty minutes. They were covered in dirt, but the car was ready to go. 

"Okay, my bad," Sarah admitted, starting the engine. "No more 'shortcuts'. Let’s stick to the main road."

"Agreed," Leo said, handing her a bottle of water. "And I’ll navigate from my phone using offline maps."

They arrived at the beach two hours late, but they were laughing about the adventure. They realized that a road trip isn’t about a perfect plan; it’s about how you handle the unexpected detours together.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Why did Sarah decide to take a dirt road?
2.‎What happened ten minutes after they left the main road?
3.‎How did the friends resolve the problem with the car?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, the friends were on a... ⁦(road trip)⁩ to the coast.
The GPS signal disappeared, and Sarah decided to take a... ⁦(shortcut)⁩.
Soon, they got a... ⁦(flat tyre)⁩ on a narrow dirt road.
Leo started to complain, but Mike told them to stop... ⁦(arguing)⁩.
They worked together to... ⁦(resolve)⁩ the issue and change the wheel.
Sarah admitted her mistake and agreed to stick to the... ⁦(main road)⁩.
Leo offered to... ⁦(navigate)⁩ using offline maps.
They arrived late but learned that handling... ⁦(detours)⁩ together is what matters.

Тема: № 123 Проживание в Airbnb и общение с хозяином жилья ⁦(Staying in an Airbnb and communicating with the host)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎host [həʊst] — хозяин, арендодатель
2.‎check-in [ˈtʃek ɪn] — заселение
3.‎key code [kiː kəʊd] — код от замка
4.‎amenities [əˈmiːnətiz] — удобства
5.‎Wi-Fi password [ˈwaɪ faɪ ˈpɑːswɜːd] — пароль от Wi-Fi
6.‎to troubleshoot [ˈtrʌblʃuːt] — устранять неполадки
7.‎review [rɪˈvjuː] — отзыв
8.‎accommodation [əˌkɒməˈdeɪʃn] — жилье, размещение

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~270 слов.
Emma arrived at the Airbnb apartment in Rome at 11 PM. She was exhausted and just wanted to sleep. She walked up to the front door and looked for the lockbox.

"Okay, the message said the key code is 4582," she muttered, pressing the buttons. 

The light blinked red. Access denied. 

She tried again. Red light. 

"Oh no," Emma sighed. She pulled out her phone and opened the Airbnb app to message her host, Marco. 

"Hi Marco, I’m at the apartment, but the key code isn’t working. Could you please help?"

She waited nervously. It was late, and she didn’t want to be stuck on the street. 

Two minutes later, her phone buzzed. 

"Hi Emma! I’m so sorry. I gave you the old code by mistake. The new code is 7739. Also, the Wi-Fi password is on the fridge. Let me know if you need anything else!"

Emma typed in 7739. The light turned green, and the door clicked open. 

"Thank you so much, Marco!" she replied. 

Inside, the accommodation was clean and cozy, exactly like the photos. She found the Wi-Fi password, connected her phone, and collapsed onto the comfortable bed. 

The next morning, the hot water stopped working. Emma messaged Marco again. Instead of panicking, he immediately called a plumber and offered to deduct 20% from her stay as compensation for the inconvenience. 

By the afternoon, everything was fixed. When it was time to leave, Emma happily left a 5-star review. "Marco is a superhost! He responds instantly and truly cares about his guests."

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎What problem did Emma face when she arrived at the apartment?
2.‎How quickly did the host respond to her message?
3.‎What happened the next morning, and how did the host handle it?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Emma arrived late and tried to... ⁦(check-in)⁩ using the lockbox.
The... ⁦(key code)⁩ she had didn't work.
She messaged her... ⁦(host)⁩, Marco, through the app.
He quickly replied with the correct code and the... ⁦(Wi-Fi password)⁩.
The next morning, the hot water stopped working, but Marco helped to... ⁦(troubleshoot)⁩ it.
He even offered a discount for the inconvenience with the... ⁦(amenities)⁩.

Тема: № 124 Ориентация в общественном транспорте незнакомого города ⁦(Navigating public transportation in a foreign city)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎subway [ˈsʌbweɪ] — метро
2.‎ticket machine [ˈtɪkɪt məˈʃiːn] — автомат по продаже билетов
3.‎route [ruːt] — маршрут
4.‎to get off [ɡet ɒf] — выходить (из транспорта)
5.‎transfer [ˈtrænsfɜː] — пересадка
6.‎map [mæp] — карта
7.‎confused [kənˈfjuːzd] — растерянный, сбитый с толку
8.‎to ask for directions [ɑːsk fə dɪˈrekʃnz] — спрашивать дорогу

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
Tom stood in the busy Tokyo subway station, staring at the colorful map on the wall. He needed to get to the Shinjuku district, but the map was entirely in Japanese. 

"Okay, don’t panic," he told himself. "Find the English letters."

He walked to the ticket machine, selected the English language option, and bought a ticket for the Yamanote Line. 

He boarded the train, feeling proud of himself. But after three stops, he looked out the window and realized something was wrong. The signs were for a completely different line. 

"I took the wrong train," he muttered, feeling completely confused. 

The train stopped, and Tom quickly got off. He looked around the platform. A friendly-looking elderly woman was waiting for her train. 

"Excuse me," Tom said, bowing slightly. "Do you speak English?"

The woman smiled warmly. "A little bit. Can I help you?"

"Yes, please. I need to go to Shinjuku, but I think I’m on the wrong route."

"Ah, Shinjuku," she said, pointing down the platform. "You need to go back two stops and make a transfer to the Chuo Line. It’s the orange one."

"Two stops back, then transfer to the orange line. Thank you so much!" Tom said, bowing again.

He followed her instructions carefully. He bought a new ticket, made the transfer, and finally saw the sign for Shinjuku. 

When he walked out of the station into the bright city streets, he let out a long breath. Navigating public transport in a foreign city was challenging, but asking for directions and staying calm made all the difference.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Why was Tom confused at the subway station?
2.‎How did Tom realize he was on the wrong train?
3.‎What advice did the elderly woman give him?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Tom was in a Tokyo... ⁦(subway)⁩ station and looked at the map.
He used the English option on the... ⁦(ticket machine)⁩ to buy a ticket.
He boarded the train but soon realized he was on the wrong... ⁦(route)⁩.
Feeling... ⁦(confused)⁩, he decided to get off at the next stop.
He decided to... ⁦(ask for directions)⁩ from an elderly woman.
She told him to go back two stops and make a... ⁦(transfer)⁩.
Tom followed her instructions and finally arrived in... ⁦(Shinjuku)⁩.
He learned that staying calm and asking for help is the best way to... ⁦(navigate)⁩.

Тема: № 125 Культурные недоразумения и языковой барьер за границей ⁦(Cultural faux pas and language barriers while traveling)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎faux pas [ˌfəʊ ˈpɑː] — оплошность, бестактность
2.‎gesture [ˈdʒestʃə] — жест
3.‎to offend [əˈfend] — обижать, оскорблять
4.‎misunderstanding [ˌmɪsʌndəˈstændɪŋ] — недоразумение
5.‎to apologize [əˈpɒlədʒaɪz] — извиняться
6.‎custom [ˈkʌstəm] — обычай, традиция
7.‎polite [pəˈlaɪt] — вежливый
8.‎to learn [lɜːn] — учить, узнавать

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~270 слов.
Mark was enjoying his first dinner in a traditional restaurant in Bangkok. The food was amazing, and the atmosphere was lively. 

When the waiter brought his bill, Mark wanted to show his appreciation. In his home country, giving a thumbs-up meant "great job." So, he smiled and gave the waiter a big thumbs-up.

The waiter’s smile instantly disappeared. He looked confused and slightly offended, then walked away quickly without saying a word.

Mark turned to his friend, Lisa. "What did I do? Was the food bad?"

Lisa shook her head, trying not to laugh. "Mark, in Thailand, the thumbs-up gesture can be considered rude, like giving someone the middle finger in the West. It was a cultural faux pas."

Mark’s eyes widened in horror. "Oh no! I didn’t mean to offend him. I thought I was being polite!"

"Don’t worry," Lisa said. "It’s a common misunderstanding for tourists. Just go and apologize."

Mark stood up, walked to the waiter, and put his hands together in a traditional 'wai' gesture, bowing his head slightly. 

"I am so sorry," Mark said slowly. "I am a tourist. I did not know the custom. The food was excellent."

The waiter’s face softened, and he smiled warmly. "No problem, sir. Thank you. We appreciate tourists who try to learn our culture."

Mark returned to the table, feeling much better. "That was a close call," he whispered.

"Indeed," Lisa agreed. "But that’s the best part of traveling. You make mistakes, you apologize, and you learn something new about the world."

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎What gesture did Mark use to show his appreciation to the waiter?
2.‎Why did the waiter get offended?
3.‎How did Mark apologize for his mistake?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Mark was having dinner in a traditional restaurant in... ⁦(Bangkok)⁩.
He gave the waiter a thumbs-up... ⁦(gesture)⁩ to show the food was good.
However, this is considered rude there, and the waiter felt... ⁦(offended)⁩.
Lisa explained that it was a cultural... ⁦(faux pas)⁩.
Mark felt terrible and didn't want to... ⁦(misunderstand)⁩ the local customs.
He went to the waiter and used a traditional bow to... ⁦(apologize)⁩.
The waiter smiled and accepted his... ⁦(apology)⁩.
Mark learned that being... ⁦(polite)⁩ means learning about local traditions.
