Тема: № 126 Планирование бюджетного отпуска ⁦(Planning a budget-friendly vacation)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎budget [ˈbʌdʒɪt] — бюджет
2.‎accommodation [əˌkɒməˈdeɪʃn] — жилье, размещение
3.‎flight deals [flaɪt diːlz] — выгодные предложения на авиабилеты
4.‎to save up [seɪv ʌp] — копить (деньги)
5.‎itinerary [aɪˈtɪnərəri] — маршрут, план поездки
6.‎affordable [əˈfɔːdəbl] — доступный по цене
7.‎to book in advance [bʊk ɪn ədˈvɑːns] — бронировать заранее
8.‎expenses [ɪkˈspensɪz] — расходы

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
"We really need a vacation," Julia said, looking at her computer screen. "But we only have $1,000 saved up. Is that even enough?"

Her husband, David, smiled. "It’s enough if we are smart about it. We just need to plan a budget-friendly vacation."

"Where can we go for that price?" Julia asked skeptically.

"Let’s look for flight deals," David said, opening a travel website. "If we are flexible with the dates, we can find cheap tickets to Portugal."

After an hour of searching, they found round-trip tickets for $400. "Okay, that leaves $600 for accommodation and expenses," Julia noted.

"Instead of a hotel, let’s book an affordable Airbnb with a kitchen," David suggested. "That way, we can cook breakfast and some dinners instead of eating at restaurants every day."

"Good idea," Julia agreed. "I’ll make a detailed itinerary. We can focus on free activities, like hiking, visiting public beaches, and walking tours."

They booked everything in advance to avoid last-minute price hikes. They packed their own snacks for the flight and used public transport instead of taxis.

When they arrived in Lisbon, the city was beautiful. They spent their days exploring historic neighborhoods and eating delicious, cheap street food. 

On their last night, they sat by the river, watching the sunset. 

"I can’t believe we did this for under $1,000," Julia said, sipping a glass of local wine.

"See?" David smiled. "You don’t need to be rich to travel. You just need to save up, plan carefully, and be creative with your expenses."

Julia nodded. It was the best, most stress-free vacation they had ever had.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎How much money did Julia and David have for their vacation?
2.‎How did they save money on accommodation and food?
3.‎What kind of activities did they include in their itinerary?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Julia and David wanted a vacation but only had a limited... ⁦(budget)⁩.
David suggested looking for... ⁦(flight deals)⁩ to Portugal.
They decided to book an... ⁦(affordable)⁩ Airbnb with a kitchen to cook meals.
This helped them reduce their daily... ⁦(expenses)⁩ on restaurants.
They made a detailed... ⁦(itinerary)⁩ focusing on free activities like hiking.
They made sure to... ⁦(book in advance)⁩ to avoid high prices.
During the trip, they used public transport and enjoyed the city.
They realized that you can travel well if you... ⁦(save up)⁩ and plan carefully.

Тема: № 127 Организация званого ужина для друзей ⁦(Hosting a dinner party for friends)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩

2.‎to invite [ɪnˈvaɪt] — приглашать
3.‎dietary restrictions [ˈdaɪətəri rɪˈstrɪkʃnz] — диетические ограничения
4.‎to prepare [prɪˈpeə] — готовить, подготавливать
5.‎dessert [dɪˈzɜːt] — десерт
6.‎to spill [spɪl] — проливать
7.‎to laugh it off [lɑːf ɪt ɒf] — отшутиться, легко отнестись к ошибке
8.‎atmosphere [ˈætməsfɪə] — атмосфера

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
Sarah was nervous. Tonight, she was hosting a dinner party for four of her closest friends. She had spent all afternoon preparing the food.

"Is the vegetarian pasta ready?" she asked her roommate, Tom, who was setting the table.

"Yes, it’s in the oven. And I remembered that Mark is allergic to nuts, so I double-checked the dessert ingredients," Tom replied.

"Perfect. Thank you for helping with the dietary restrictions," Sarah said, wiping her forehead. 

At 7 PM, the doorbell rang. Her friends arrived, bringing a bottle of wine and a bouquet of flowers. The atmosphere was immediately warm and cheerful. 

They sat down to eat. The pasta was delicious, and everyone was chatting and laughing. Sarah felt proud of her cooking. 

Then, disaster struck. As Sarah reached for the salt, her elbow knocked over a full glass of red wine. It spilled all over the white tablecloth and onto the floor.

"Oh no!" Sarah gasped, freezing in panic. "I’m so sorry, everyone!"

For a second, there was silence. Then, Mark grabbed some napkins. "Don’t worry, Sarah! It’s just a tablecloth. We can laugh it off."

"Here, let me help," Lisa said, rushing to the kitchen for a towel. 

Within two minutes, the spill was cleaned up, and Tom brought out a fresh tablecloth. 

"See?" Tom smiled. "No harm done. Now, who wants dessert?"

The rest of the evening was perfect. Sarah realized that hosting isn’t about everything being flawless. It’s about the people, the conversation, and the ability to laugh when things go wrong.

Тема: № 128 Присутствие на свадьбе друга (и произнесение тоста) ⁦(Attending a friend's wedding and giving a toast)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎wedding [ˈwedɪŋ] — свадьба
2.‎groom [ɡruːm] — жених
3.‎bride [braɪd] — невеста
4.‎speech [spiːtʃ] — речь
5.‎toast [təʊst] — тост
6.‎nervous [ˈnɜːvəs] — нервничающий
7.‎to memorize [ˈmeməraɪz] — заучивать наизусть
8.‎heartfelt [ˈhɑːtfelt] — искренний, сердечный

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
Leo adjusted his tie for the tenth time. He was the best man at his best friend David’s wedding, and it was almost time for his speech. 

"You look like you’re going to be sick," David said, walking over with a smile. 

"I am," Leo admitted. "Public speaking is my biggest fear. What if I forget my lines? What if I make the bride cry?"

David laughed and patted him on the shoulder. "Just speak from the heart. Everyone here loves you. You don’t need to memorize every single word."

"Easy for you to say. You’re not the one holding the microphone," Leo sighed. 

He pulled a small piece of paper from his pocket. He had written down a few key points and one funny story about their college days. 

An hour later, the music stopped, and the DJ announced, "And now, a few words from the best man, Leo."

Leo walked to the front of the room. His hands were shaking slightly as he took the microphone. He looked at David and his new wife, Sarah, who were smiling warmly at him. 

"Hi everyone," Leo began, his voice a bit shaky at first. "For those who don’t know me, I’m Leo. I’ve known David since we were ten."

He took a deep breath and looked at his notes. He told the funny story about their first road trip. The room erupted in laughter. The tension left his body. 

"To David and Sarah," Leo said, raising his glass. "May your life together be as amazing as you both are. Cheers!"

"Cheers!" the guests shouted, clapping loudly.

Leo returned to his seat, feeling a massive wave of relief. He hadn’t been perfect, but his heartfelt words had made the moment truly special.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)

3.‎How did the guests react to Leo’s speech?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:

He started his... ⁦(speech)⁩ with a funny story about their college days.
The guests laughed, and Leo felt the tension... ⁦(leave)⁩ his body.
He raised his glass for a... ⁦(toast)⁩ to the happy couple.

Тема: № 129 Вступление в местный клуб (книжный, беговой и т.д.) ⁦(Joining a local club or community group)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎local club [ˈləʊkl klʌb] — местный клуб
2.‎to join [dʒɔɪn] — вступать, присоединяться
3.‎hobby [ˈhɒbi] — хобби, увлечение
4.‎to attend [əˈtend] — посещать
5.‎regular member [ˈreɡjələ ˈmembə] — постоянный участник
6.‎to socialize [ˈsəʊʃəlaɪz] — общаться
7.‎beginner [bɪˈɡɪnə] — новичок
8.‎welcoming [ˈwelkəmɪŋ] — гостеприимный, дружелюбный

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~270 слов.
Mark stood outside the community center, feeling a bit nervous. He had decided to join the local running club, but he hadn't run in five years. 

"First time?" a cheerful voice asked. 

Mark turned to see a woman in bright running shoes. "Is it that obvious?" he laughed. 

"A little," she smiled. "I'm Sarah. Don't worry, we have groups for all speeds. Are you a beginner?"

"Definitely," Mark admitted. "I just want to get fit and maybe make some friends. My main hobby used to be sitting on the couch."

Sarah laughed. "Well, you're in the right place. We attend meetings every Tuesday and Thursday. The best part isn't the running; it's the chance to socialize afterward."

They walked inside together. The group was already gathering. A tall man named David, a regular member, came over and handed Mark a club T-shirt. 

"Welcome, Mark! We start with a ten-minute warm-up, then a slow two-mile run. If you need to walk, just walk. No pressure," David explained.

The run was harder than Mark expected, but nobody left him behind. Sarah ran beside him, chatting about their favorite local cafes. When they finished, the whole group went to a nearby coffee shop. 

Mark sat with a warm latte, feeling tired but happy. He realized that joining the club wasn't just about exercise. It was about finding a welcoming community that shared his new interest. He couldn't wait for Thursday.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)

During the run, Sarah stayed with him and they talked about their... ⁦(hobby)⁩.
Afterward, the group went to a coffee shop, which felt very... ⁦(welcoming)⁩.
Mark realized the club was a great... ⁦(community)⁩.

5.‎frustrating [ˈfrʌstreɪtɪŋ] — разочаровывающий, раздражающий
6.‎to improve [ɪmˈpruːv] — улучшаться, совершенствоваться
7.‎lesson [ˈlesn] — урок
8.‎progress [ˈprəʊɡres] — прогресс, продвижение

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~275 слов.
Emma stared at the acoustic guitar in her living room. She had bought it a month ago, dreaming of playing her favorite songs. But her fingers hurt, and the chords sounded terrible.

"This is so frustrating," she muttered, putting the guitar back in its case. 

Her teacher, Mr. Davis, noticed her disappointment during their next lesson. "Having trouble with the F chord?" he asked gently.

"My fingers feel like they're going to break," Emma sighed. "Maybe I'm too old to learn a new musical instrument. Kids learn so fast."

Mr. Davis smiled. "Emma, adults have an advantage: patience and discipline. Kids quit when it gets hard. You just need to practice a little bit every day, not for hours at a time."

"How much is 'a little bit'?" she asked.

"Fifteen minutes," he replied. "Just fifteen minutes of focused practice. Don't worry about playing a whole song yet. Just focus on switching between two chords smoothly."

Emma decided to try his advice. That evening, she set a timer for fifteen minutes. She practiced switching from C to G. It was clumsy at first, but by the end of the timer, her fingers moved a bit faster. 

She did this every day after work. Two weeks later, during her lesson, she played a simple song from start to finish without stopping. 

"Wow," Mr. Davis said, clapping. "Look at your progress!"

Emma smiled, looking at her calloused fingertips. They didn't hurt anymore. She realized that learning as an adult wasn't about natural talent; it was about consistency and giving yourself permission to be a beginner.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Why did Emma want to put her guitar away?
2.‎What advantage did Mr. Davis say adults have over kids?
3.‎How long did Emma practice each day according to the teacher's advice?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Emma bought a... ⁦(guitar)⁩ but found it hard to play.
She thought learning a... ⁦(musical instrument)⁩ as an adult was too difficult.
Her teacher told her that adults have... ⁦(patience)⁩ and discipline.
He advised her to... ⁦(practice)⁩ for just fifteen minutes a day.
She focused on switching between two... ⁦(chords)⁩ smoothly.
After two weeks, she could play a simple song without... ⁦(stopping)⁩.
Her teacher praised her... ⁦(progress)⁩.
Emma learned that consistency is the key to... ⁦(improve)⁩.

Тема: № 131 Создание домашнего сада или уход за комнатными растениями ⁦(Starting a home garden or caring for houseplants)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎houseplant [ˈhaʊsplɑːnt] — комнатное растение
2.‎to water [ˈwɔːtə] — поливать
3.‎sunlight [ˈsʌnlaɪt] — солнечный свет
4.‎to repot [ˌriːˈpɒt] — пересаживать (в другой горшок)
5.‎soil [sɔɪl] — почва, грунт
6.‎to thrive [θraɪv] — процветать, хорошо расти
7.‎overwater [ˌəʊvəˈwɔːtə] — переливать (растение)
8.‎patience [ˈpeɪʃns] — терпение

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
Tom proudly placed a large Monstera plant in the corner of his living room. "You're going to make this place look amazing," he told it. 

For the first week, Tom watered it every single day. He wanted it to grow fast. But soon, the large green leaves started to turn yellow and droop. 

"Oh no, what did I do?" Tom panicked. He called his friend Lisa, who had a jungle of plants in her apartment.

"Let me see," Lisa said over a video call. She looked closely at the screen. "Tom, the soil is soaking wet. Are you watering it every day?"

"Yes, I want it to be happy," Tom admitted.

Lisa laughed gently. "You're loving it to death. Most houseplants prefer to dry out a bit between waterings. You are overwatering it, and the roots are rotting."

"Can I save it?" Tom asked, worried.

"Yes, but you need to repot it," Lisa explained. "Get some fresh, well-draining soil and a pot with holes at the bottom. And move it to a spot with bright, indirect sunlight."

Tom went to the garden center immediately. He carefully took the plant out, trimmed the bad roots, and put it in new soil. He placed it near the window and made a strict rule: water only when the top inch of soil felt dry. 

It took a month, but slowly, a new, bright green leaf unfurled. 

"Look at that!" Tom texted Lisa with a photo. 

"See?" she replied. "Plants don't need constant attention. They just need the right conditions and a little patience."

Tom smiled, finally understanding that caring for a plant was about balance, not just pouring water.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Why did Tom's Monstera leaves start to turn yellow?
2.‎What mistake was Tom making with the plant?
3.‎What advice did Lisa give him to save the plant?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Tom bought a large... ⁦(houseplant)⁩ for his living room.
He decided to... ⁦(water)⁩ it every single day.
Soon, the leaves turned yellow because he started to... ⁦(overwater)⁩ it.
His friend Lisa explained that the... ⁦(soil)⁩ was too wet.
She told him to... ⁦(repot)⁩ the plant into a new pot with holes.
He moved it to a place with good... ⁦(sunlight)⁩.
Tom learned to wait until the soil was dry before watering again.
With a little... ⁦(patience)⁩, the plant started to... ⁦(thrive)⁩.

Тема: № 132 Волонтерство в местном приюте для животных или food bank ⁦(Volunteering at a local shelter or food bank)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎to volunteer [ˌvɒlənˈtɪə] — работать волонтером
2.‎animal shelter [ˈænɪml ˈʃeltə] — приют для животных
3.‎to walk [wɔːk] — выгуливать
4.‎to clean [kliːn] — убирать, чистить
5.‎rewarding [rɪˈwɔːdɪŋ] — приносящий удовлетворение
6.‎donation [dəʊˈneɪʃn] — пожертвование
7.‎to adopt [əˈdɒpt] — забрать (животное) домой
8.‎community [kəˈmjuːnəti] — сообщество

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~275 слов.
"I thought volunteering would be just playing with puppies," Kate said, scrubbing a concrete floor at the local animal shelter. 

Her friend, Ben, laughed, handing her a mop. "It is playing with puppies. But first, you have to clean their cages. They can't be adopted if their space isn't clean."

Kate sighed but kept scrubbing. She had signed up to volunteer every Saturday morning. It was hard work, and she smelled like bleach, but she loved being part of the community effort. 

After an hour of cleaning, the shelter manager, Mrs. Davis, handed Kate a leash. "You've earned this. This is Barnaby. He’s been here for three months and is very shy. He needs a gentle walk."

Kate walked into the kennel. Barnaby, a medium-sized mixed breed, hid in the back corner. Kate sat on the floor and waited quietly. Slowly, Barnaby crept forward and sniffed her hand. 

"Hey, buddy," Kate whispered. "Let's go for a walk."

They walked around the shelter's fenced yard. Barnaby didn't pull on the leash; he just stayed close to Kate's side. For twenty minutes, it was just the two of them. 

When they returned, a family was looking at the dogs. The little girl pointed at Barnaby. "That one, Mom! The quiet one."

Kate watched as the family filled out the adoption papers. Barnaby was finally going home. 

"See?" Ben said, bumping her shoulder. "Tired and smelly, but worth it."

Kate smiled, watching Barnaby leave. "Absolutely. It’s the most rewarding feeling in the world."

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎What was Kate's initial expectation about volunteering?
2.‎Why did Kate have to clean the cages first?
3.‎What happened to the shy dog, Barnaby, at the end of the day?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:

She realized she had to... ⁦(clean)⁩ the cages before playing with the dogs.
After cleaning, the manager gave her a leash to... ⁦(walk)⁩ a shy dog named Barnaby.
Kate sat quietly and gained the dog's... ⁦(trust)⁩.
Later, a family came and decided to... ⁦(adopt)⁩ Barnaby.
Kate watched them fill out the... ⁦(papers)⁩.
Even though the work was hard, she felt it was very... ⁦(rewarding)⁩.
She felt proud to help her... ⁦(community)⁩.

Тема: № 133 Реальность жизни цифрового кочевника или работы из кафе ⁦(The reality of being a digital nomad or working from a cafe)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎digital nomad [ˈdɪdʒɪtl ˈnəʊmæd] — цифровой кочевник
2.‎cafe [ˈkæfeɪ] — кафе
3.‎Wi-Fi [ˈwaɪ faɪ] — вай-фай, беспроводной интернет
4.‎distraction [dɪˈstrækʃn] — отвлечение
5.‎noisy [ˈnɔɪzi] — шумный
6.‎to focus [ˈfəʊkəs] — сосредотачиваться
7.‎laptop [ˈlæptɒp] — ноутбук
8.‎environment [ɪnˈvaɪərənmənt] — окружающая среда, обстановка

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
Alex sat at a small table in a trendy cafe, his laptop open. He had been working as a digital nomad for a month, and he thought it would be like the photos online: sipping a latte while typing genius code.

The reality was different. 

"Can I get a large oat milk cappuccino?" the person next to him shouted over the loud indie music. 

Alex sighed and put on his noise-canceling headphones. He needed to focus on a project deadline, but the environment was chaotic. 

Then, his screen froze. The Wi-Fi symbol showed only one bar. 

"No, no, no," Alex muttered, refreshing the page. "Not now."

His colleague, Sarah, called him on Slack. "Hey, are you in the meeting? We can't hear you."

"My Wi-Fi is terrible," Alex typed back. "I'm going to find a quieter corner."

He packed his laptop and moved to a small, empty booth at the back of the cafe. It was darker, but the internet was stronger, and the noise was muffled. He ordered a simple black coffee and finally started working. 

Two hours later, he finished the task. He stretched and looked around. The cafe was still busy, but he had managed to get things done. 

Later, he met a friend who also worked remotely. "Is the digital nomad life amazing?" she asked.

"It is," Alex smiled. "But it’s not all glamorous. You have to learn how to handle distractions, find good Wi-Fi, and create your own productive environment wherever you are."

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)

2.‎What two problems did Alex face while trying to work?
3.‎How did Alex solve the problem and finish his task?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:

He sat in a... ⁦(cafe)⁩ with his... ⁦(laptop)⁩ open.
However, the place was very... ⁦(noisy)⁩ and full of distractions.
His... ⁦(Wi-Fi)⁩ connection was terrible, and his screen froze.
He had to move to a quiet booth at the back to... ⁦(focus)⁩.
He managed to finish his project despite the difficult... ⁦(environment)⁩.
He realized that remote work requires adaptability and finding a good... ⁦(connection)⁩.
It is not always perfect, but it is still... ⁦(amazing)⁩.

Тема: № 134 Контроль экранного времени у детей и гаджет-зависимость ⁦(Managing screen time and technology for children)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎screen time [skriːn taɪm] — время, проведенное у экрана
2.‎gadget [ˈɡædʒɪt] — гаджет, электронное устройство
3.‎to limit [ˈlɪmɪt] — ограничивать
4.‎tablet [ˈtæblət] — планшет
5.‎bedtime [ˈbedtaɪm] — время отхода ко сну
6.‎alternative [ɔːlˈtɜːnətɪv] — альтернатива, замена
7.‎outdoor activity [ˈaʊtdɔː ækˈtɪvəti] — активность на свежем воздухе
8.‎agreement [əˈɡriːmənt] — соглашение, договоренность

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~275 слов.
"Leo, it's 8 PM. Tablet down, please," Anna said, walking into the living room. 

Her eight-year-old son groaned. "But I'm in the middle of a level! Just five more minutes."

"We had an agreement," Anna said firmly but gently. "No gadgets one hour before bedtime. It makes it hard for you to sleep."

Leo crossed his arms. "But there's nothing else to do!"

Anna thought for a moment. She knew that just taking the tablet away would lead to a fight. She needed to offer a good alternative. 

"Okay," she said. "How about we build the new Lego set together? Or we can play a board game."

Leo hesitated. "The spaceship one?"

"Yes, the spaceship one," Anna smiled. "And after that, we can read a chapter of your new book."

Leo put the tablet on the coffee table. "Okay. But you have to be the bad guy in the game."

"Deal," Anna laughed. 

They spent the next forty-five minutes building the Lego spaceship. Leo was fully engaged, laughing and talking. He didn't ask for his tablet once. 

When it was time for bed, Leo was calm and ready. "That was fun, Mom," he said, yawning. 

"I'm glad you liked it," Anna replied, tucking him in. "We can do it again tomorrow."

Anna closed his door and let out a quiet sigh of relief. Limiting screen time wasn't easy, but she realized that children don't just need rules; they need engaging alternatives and their parents' time. 

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Why did Anna ask Leo to put his tablet away?
2.‎What alternative did Anna offer to Leo?
3.‎How did Leo react to the new activity?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Anna told Leo it was... ⁦(bedtime)⁩ and he had to stop using his device.
She reminded him of their... ⁦(agreement)⁩ about no gadgets before sleep.
Leo complained, but Anna offered a fun... ⁦(alternative)⁩.
They decided to build a Lego... ⁦(spaceship)⁩ together.
Leo forgot about his... ⁦(tablet)⁩ and enjoyed the activity.
He was calm and ready for... ⁦(sleep)⁩ afterward.
Anna realized that kids need engaging... ⁦(outdoor activity)⁩ or games, not just rules.
Managing... ⁦(screen time)⁩ requires patience and creativity.

Тема: № 135 Настройка умного дома и первые технические сбои ⁦(Setting up a smart home and dealing with first tech glitches)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎smart home [smɑːt həʊm] — умный дом
2.‎voice assistant [vɔɪs əˈsɪstənt] — голосовой помощник
3.‎to connect [kəˈnekt] — подключать
4.‎glitch [ɡlɪtʃ] — сбой, неполадка
5.‎to reset [ˌriːˈset] — перезагружать, сбрасывать настройки
6.‎frustrated [ˈfrʌstreɪtɪd] — расстроенный, раздраженный
7.‎convenient [kənˈviːniənt] — удобный
8.‎manual [ˈmænjuəl] — руководство, инструкция

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
David stood in his living room, holding a small, round smart speaker. "This is going to be so convenient," he told his wife, Emma. "I'll be able to control the lights with my voice."

"Good luck with that," Emma smiled, drinking her tea. "Technology never works the first time."

David plugged in the device and opened the app on his phone. "Okay, let's connect to the Wi-Fi." 

He followed the steps. The app searched for the device, but after a minute, it showed an error message: "Connection failed."

"Come on," David muttered, feeling frustrated. He tried again. Same error. 

"Did you read the manual?" Emma asked, not looking up from her book.

"Who reads the manual?" David replied. But after the third failed attempt, he sighed, picked up the small booklet, and started reading. 

"Ah," he said. "It says here I need to reset my router first if the network is 5GHz."

David walked to the hallway, unplugged the router, waited ten seconds, and plugged it back in. He waited for the lights to turn green. 

He went back to the living room and tried the setup again. This time, the app connected instantly. 

"Hello, David," the voice assistant said cheerfully. "I am ready."

David grinned. "Turn off the living room lights."

The lights instantly dimmed. 

"Okay, that is pretty cool," Emma admitted, looking up. 

David laughed. "It is. But you were right. The manual actually helps." He realized that setting up a smart home wasn't magic; it was just following instructions and staying calm when a small glitch appeared.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)

He discovered he needed to... ⁦(reset)⁩ the router first.
After restarting the router, the... ⁦(voice assistant)⁩ connected successfully.

Тема: № 136 Удаленное обучение или прохождение онлайн-курса ⁦(Taking an online course or remote learning experience)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎online course [ˌɒnlaɪn kɔːs] — онлайн-курс
2.‎to enroll [ɪnˈrəʊl] — записываться, поступать
3.‎deadline [ˈdedlaɪn] — крайний срок
4.‎video lecture [ˈvɪdiəʊ ˌlektʃə] — видеолекция
5.‎discussion forum [dɪˈskʌʃn ˈfɔːrəm] — форум для обсуждений
6.‎self-discipline [ˌself ˈdɪsəplɪn] — самодисциплина
7.‎certificate [səˈtɪfɪkət] — сертификат
8.‎to complete [kəmˈpliːt] — завершать, проходить до конца

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~275 слов.
Anna stared at her computer screen. She had decided to enroll in an online course for graphic design. The first week was exciting, but by week three, the reality of remote learning set in.

"I should be watching this video lecture," she thought, looking at the paused screen. "But the sofa is so comfortable, and my phone is right here."

She checked her phone. No new messages. She sighed and realized she was procrastinating. The final project deadline was in two weeks, and she had barely started.

"Remote learning requires serious self-discipline," she muttered to herself. 

She decided to make a change. She moved her laptop to the kitchen table, poured a cup of coffee, and put her phone in another room. 

She also logged into the course's discussion forum. She had been avoiding it, but now she typed a message: "Hi everyone, I'm struggling with the logo design module. Is anyone free for a virtual study session this weekend?"

Within an hour, three classmates replied. On Saturday, they met on a video call. They shared their screens, gave each other feedback, and motivated each other to keep going. 

"That was so helpful," Anna said at the end of the call. "I thought I was the only one finding this hard."

"We all do," her classmate Mark replied. "That's why we have the forum."

With her new study routine and the support of her classmates, Anna finished her project two days before the deadline. A month later, a PDF arrived in her email: her completion certificate. 

She printed it and put it on her wall. It was proof that with self-discipline and a little help from others, she could achieve her goals.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Why was Anna procrastinating in the third week of her course?
2.‎What two changes did Anna make to improve her studying?
3.‎How did the virtual study session help Anna?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:

She moved to the kitchen table and hid her phone to... ⁦(focus)⁩.
She also posted a message on the... ⁦(discussion forum)⁩.
Her classmates agreed to a virtual study... ⁦(session)⁩.
They helped each other and shared... ⁦(feedback)⁩.
Anna managed to... ⁦(complete)⁩ her project before the... ⁦(deadline)⁩.
She proudly displayed her... ⁦(certificate)⁩ on the wall.

Тема: № 137 Синдром самозванца на новой должности ⁦(Dealing with imposter syndrome at a new job)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩

8.‎mindset [ˈmaɪndset] — образ мыслей, настрой

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
Mark sat at his new desk, staring at the complex project brief. It was his second week as a senior manager, and he felt a familiar knot in his stomach. 

"I have no idea what I'm doing," he whispered to himself. "They made a mistake hiring me. I'm not qualified for this."

His colleague, Sarah, walked by with a cup of coffee. "You look like you're solving a murder mystery, Mark. Everything okay?"

Mark sighed. "Honestly? I feel like a fraud. Everyone here is so smart, and I’m just waiting for someone to realize I don't belong here."

Sarah pulled up a chair. "Ah, imposter syndrome. I had it for six months when I started. Let me tell you a secret: they didn't hire you by mistake. They saw your achievements at your old company."

"But what if I can't handle this project?" Mark asked, still doubting himself.

"Then you ask for help," Sarah said firmly. "No one expects you to know everything on day fourteen. Have you checked the feedback from your last presentation?"

Mark nodded slowly. "The director said it was excellent."

"Exactly! You are letting a negative mindset trick you. You earned this role. Just take it one task at a time."

Mark took a deep breath. He realized Sarah was right. He opened his email, wrote down three small goals for the day, and asked Sarah a specific question about the project. 

By the end of the week, he had finished the first draft. It wasn't perfect, but it was solid. He learned that feeling like an imposter is normal, but letting it stop you is a choice. He was qualified, and he was finally starting to believe it.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Why did Mark feel like a fraud at his new job?
2.‎What secret did Sarah share about imposter syndrome?
3.‎What small steps did Mark take to overcome his doubts?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Mark felt he wasn't... ⁦(qualified)⁩ for his new senior manager role.
He suffered from... ⁦(imposter syndrome)⁩ and thought he didn't belong.
Sarah reminded him of his past... ⁦(achievements)⁩ and positive... ⁦(feedback)⁩.
She told him that asking for help is a normal part of the job.
Mark decided to change his... ⁦(mindset)⁩ and take things one task at a time.
He finished the first draft of the project by the end of the... ⁦(week)⁩.
He realized that feeling like a fraud is normal, but he truly... ⁦(earned)⁩ his role.

Тема: № 138 Прощание с коллегой, который уезжает в другую страну ⁦(Saying goodbye to a colleague who is relocating)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎to relocate [ˌriːləʊˈkeɪt] — переезжать (в другую страну/город)
2.‎farewell party [ˈfeəwel ˈpɑːti] — прощальная вечеринка
3.‎to appreciate [əˈpriːʃieɪt] — ценить
4.‎to keep in touch [kiːp ɪn tʌtʃ] — поддерживать связь
5.‎memorable [ˈmemərəbl] — незабываемый
6.‎speech [spiːtʃ] — речь
7.‎bittersweet [ˈbɪtəswiːt] — горько-сладкий (о чувствах)
8.‎colleague [ˈkɒliːɡ] — коллега

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~275 слов.
The break room was decorated with balloons and a large banner that read: "Good luck in London, Emma!" 

Emma stood by the cake, smiling, though her eyes were a bit watery. After five years at the company, she was relocating to the UK for a new opportunity. 

"We’re going to miss you terribly," said David, her team leader, handing her a wrapped gift. "You’ve been the heart of this department."

"Thank you, David," Emma said, genuinely touched. "I’ve appreciated every single day here. You all made my work feel like a second family."

Her coworker, Lisa, stepped forward with a glass of juice. "I can’t believe you’re actually leaving. Who am I going to complain about the coffee with?"

Emma laughed. "You’ll manage. And we have WhatsApp. I expect weekly updates on office drama."

"Deal," Lisa smiled. 

David tapped his spoon against his glass. "Alright everyone, a quick speech. Emma, your dedication and creativity have made a huge impact on this team. We are sad to see you go, but incredibly proud of your new adventure. Cheers to Emma!"

"Cheers!" the room echoed. 

Emma felt a bittersweet lump in her throat. She was excited about London, but leaving these people was harder than she expected. 

Later, as people were leaving, Lisa hugged her tightly. "Don't be a stranger. We must keep in touch."

"I promise," Emma said. She looked around the familiar office one last time. It was a memorable day, filled with laughter, good wishes, and the promise of new beginnings. She was ready for London, but she would always cherish the time she spent here.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)

2.‎How did Emma feel about leaving her colleagues?
3.‎What did David say in his short speech?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:

Emma was... ⁦(relocating)⁩ to London after five years at the company.
She told her colleagues how much she... ⁦(appreciated)⁩ working with them.
Lisa joked about who she would complain to now, and they agreed to... ⁦(keep in touch)⁩.
David gave a short... ⁦(speech)⁩, praising Emma's dedication.
Emma felt... ⁦(bittersweet)⁩ about leaving but excited for her new adventure.

Тема: № 139 Организация корпоратива или тимбилдинга на природе ⁦(Organizing a company retreat or team-building event)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎retreat [rɪˈtriːt] — выездное мероприятие, ретрит
2.‎team-building [ˈtiːm ˌbɪldɪŋ] — тимбилдинг, сплочение команды
3.‎outdoors [ˈaʊtdɔːz] — на свежем воздухе, на природе
4.‎to organize [ˈɔːɡənaɪz] — организовывать
5.‎activity [ækˈtɪvəti] — занятие, активность
6.‎to bond [bɒnd] — сближаться, налаживать связь
7.‎awkward [ˈɔːkwəd] — неловкий
8.‎successful [səkˈsesfl] — успешный

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
"Okay, everyone, listen up!" shouted Chloe, the HR manager, clapping her hands. "Welcome to our annual company retreat!"

The marketing team stood in a forest clearing, looking slightly awkward in their hiking boots and casual clothes. 

"Today is all about team-building," Chloe continued. "No laptops, no emails. Just us, nature, and a little friendly competition."

Mark, a senior developer, whispered to his colleague, "Please don't say we have to do trust falls."

"Worse," Chloe smiled, hearing him. "You have to build a raft using only these ropes, wooden planks, and barrels. And then you have to sail it across the lake."

A collective groan went through the group. "We don't even know how to tie a proper knot," someone complained.

"That’s the point!" Chloe said. "You have to figure it out together. You have one hour."

At first, it was chaotic. Everyone was giving different instructions, and the raft looked like a pile of junk. But then, Sarah, the quiet accountant, stepped up. 

"Okay, let's stop arguing," she said calmly. "Mark, you’re good with structure, hold the planks. David, tie the ropes here. I’ll check the balance."

Slowly, the awkwardness faded. They started listening to each other. They laughed when a barrel rolled away, and they cheered when the final knot held strong. 

An hour later, their raft was floating. It wasn't pretty, but it stayed afloat. As they paddled across the lake, cheering and splashing water, they weren't just coworkers anymore. They were a team. 

That evening, around the campfire, Mark turned to Sarah. "You were a natural leader today."

Sarah smiled. "We just needed to bond outside the office to see it." It was, without a doubt, a highly successful retreat.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎What was the main goal of the company retreat?
2.‎What specific activity did the team have to do?
3.‎How did the team's dynamic change during the activity?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Chloe welcomed the team to the annual company... ⁦(retreat)⁩.
The goal was... ⁦(team-building)⁩ without laptops or emails.
The team had to build a raft... ⁦(outdoors)⁩ using ropes and planks.
At first, the situation was chaotic and... ⁦(awkward)⁩.
Then, Sarah stepped up and helped them... ⁦(organize)⁩ the work.
They started to listen to each other and managed to... ⁦(bond)⁩.
They successfully sailed the raft across the lake.
The event was highly... ⁦(successful)⁩ because they learned to work as a real team.

Тема: № 140 Запрос на повышение зарплаты (разговор с боссом) ⁦(Negotiating a salary raise with your boss)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎salary raise [ˈsæləri reɪz] — повышение зарплаты
2.‎to negotiate [nɪˈɡəʊʃieɪt] — вести переговоры
3.‎performance [pəˈfɔːməns] — эффективность, результаты работы
4.‎achievement [əˈtʃiːvmənt] — достижение
5.‎market rate [ˈmɑːkɪt reɪt] — рыночная ставка
6.‎confident [ˈkɒnfɪdənt] — уверенный
7.‎to deserve [dɪˈzɜːv] — заслуживать
8.‎compromise [ˈkɒmprəmaɪz] — компромисс

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
Tom sat outside his boss’s office, reviewing his notes one last time. He had been with the company for two years, and he was ready to negotiate a salary raise. 

"Come in, Tom," Mr. Davis said, looking up from his computer. "What’s on your mind?"

Tom took a deep breath and sat down. "Thank you for your time, Mr. Davis. I’d like to discuss my compensation. Over the past year, my performance has been strong. I led the Q3 project to completion two weeks early, and our team’s sales increased by 15%."

Mr. Davis nodded, looking at Tom’s file. "That’s true. Your achievements have been impressive."

"Because of this increased responsibility and the current market rate for my role, I believe a 10% salary raise is fair and reflects the value I bring to the company," Tom said, keeping his voice steady and confident.

Mr. Davis leaned back. "Ten percent is a bit higher than our budget allows for this quarter, Tom. However, I agree that you deserve recognition."

Tom was prepared for this. "I understand budget constraints. Would you be open to a 7% raise now, with a performance review in six months to discuss the remaining 3%?"

Mr. Davis smiled. "That’s a very reasonable compromise. Let’s do 7% effective next month, and we will definitely revisit this in six months."

"Thank you, Mr. Davis. I appreciate it," Tom said, shaking his hand. 

Walking back to his desk, Tom felt a massive wave of relief. He realized that asking for a raise isn’t about demanding money; it’s about calmly presenting your achievements, knowing your worth, and being willing to find a fair compromise.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎What achievements did Tom mention to his boss?
2.‎Why couldn't the boss agree to the full 10% raise immediately?
3.‎What compromise did Tom suggest?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Tom prepared to... ⁦(negotiate)⁩ a salary raise with his boss.
He listed his recent... ⁦(achievements)⁩, like finishing a project early.
He asked for a 10% raise based on his... ⁦(performance)⁩ and the... ⁦(market rate)⁩.
The boss said 10% was over the current budget.
Tom stayed... ⁦(confident)⁩ and suggested a... ⁦(compromise)⁩.
They agreed on a 7% raise now and a review in six months.
Tom felt relieved and realized it's important to know what you... ⁦(deserve)⁩.

Тема: № 141 Баланс между основной работой и подработкой (фрилансом) ⁦(Balancing a full-time job with a side hustle)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎side hustle [saɪd ˈhʌsl] — подработка, побочный заработок
2.‎full-time job [fʊl taɪm dʒɒb] — работа на полную ставку
3.‎to balance [ˈbæləns] — балансировать, совмещать
4.‎exhausted [ɪɡˈzɔːstɪd] — истощенный, очень уставший
5.‎schedule [ˈʃedjuːl] — расписание, график
6.‎to prioritize [praɪˈɒrɪtaɪz] — расставлять приоритеты
7.‎burnout [ˈbɜːnaʊt] — выгорание
8.‎rewarding [rɪˈwɔːdɪŋ] — приносящий удовлетворение

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
It was 11:30 PM on a Tuesday. Julia was staring at her laptop, her eyes burning. She had just finished an eight-hour shift at her marketing job, and now she was trying to complete a freelance graphic design order.

"I can't do this anymore," she groaned, rubbing her temples. She was completely exhausted. 

Her roommate, Alex, walked into the kitchen. "You’re still working? Julia, you’re going to get burnout."

"I have to finish this side hustle project," Julia replied. "The extra money is great, but I have no life. I’m missing dinners with friends, I’m not sleeping, and I’m making mistakes at my full-time job."

Alex sat down opposite her. "The money is rewarding, but it’s not worth your health. You need to balance this better. You can't just work whenever you feel guilty about not working."

"So, what do I do? Quit?"

"No, just set boundaries," Alex advised. "Create a strict schedule. For example, no freelance work on weekends, and stop working by 9 PM on weekdays. You need to prioritize your rest."

Julia thought about it. It made sense. She was treating her free time like a second job, which was unsustainable. 

The next day, she spoke to her freelance client. "I can deliver this project by Friday, but I will only be working on it during specific evening hours. I hope that works for you."

The client agreed. Julia stuck to her new schedule. She worked from 7 PM to 9 PM, and then she closed her laptop. 

For the first time in months, she went to a Friday night dinner with friends. She felt rested, happy, and surprisingly, her freelance work improved because she was actually focused. She finally found her balance.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Why was Julia feeling exhausted at 11:30 PM?
2.‎What advice did Alex give her to avoid burnout?
3.‎How did Julia's work and life improve after she set boundaries?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Julia was working late on her... ⁦(side hustle)⁩ after her... ⁦(full-time job)⁩.
She felt completely... ⁦(exhausted)⁩ and was making mistakes.
Alex warned her about... ⁦(burnout)⁩ and told her to set boundaries.
He advised her to create a strict... ⁦(schedule)⁩ and... ⁦(prioritize)⁩ her rest.
Julia told her client she would only work during specific... ⁦(hours)⁩.
She stopped working by 9 PM and finally went out with... ⁦(friends)⁩.
She realized that resting made her freelance work more... ⁦(rewarding)⁩ and focused.

Тема: № 142 Опыт изучения нового языка во взрослом возрасте ⁦(The experience of learning a new language as an adult)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎language learner [ˈlæŋɡwɪdʒ ˈlɜːnə] — изучающий язык
2.‎accent [ˈæksent] — акцент
3.‎to practice [ˈpræktɪs] — практиковаться
4.‎embarrassing [ɪmˈbærəsɪŋ] — смущающий, неловкий
5.‎fluent [ˈfluːənt] — беглый (о языке)
6.‎progress [ˈprəʊɡres] — прогресс
7.‎consistency [kənˈsɪstənsi] — постоянство, последовательность
8.‎to encourage [ɪnˈkʌrɪdʒ] — ободрять, поощрять

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~275 слов.
David sat in the Spanish class, feeling his face turn red. The teacher had just asked him a simple question, and he had completely frozen. 

"I... uh... me gusta... the... bread?" he stammered. 

The class giggled, and David wanted to disappear. "This is so embarrassing," he thought. "I’m 40 years old. My brain isn't built for this. Kids learn languages so easily, but I sound ridiculous."

After class, the teacher, Señora Garcia, asked him to stay for a moment. 

"David, you look frustrated," she said kindly. 

"I am," he admitted. "I’ve been studying for six months, and I still can't form a basic sentence. My accent is terrible, and I’ll never be fluent."

Señora Garcia smiled warmly. "David, being an adult language learner is different, but it’s not worse. Adults have life experience and discipline. Yes, you will make mistakes. That is how you practice and improve."

"But it feels so slow," David sighed.

"Progress is not a straight line," she explained. "It’s about consistency. Fifteen minutes every day is better than three hours once a week. And your accent? It shows you have lived a life. It’s charming, not terrible."

David took a deep breath. Her words helped to encourage him. 

That evening, instead of giving up, he put on a Spanish podcast while cooking dinner. He repeated the words out loud, not caring if he sounded funny. 

A few months later, he ordered his coffee in Spanish at a local cafe. The barista smiled and replied in Spanish. It was a small victory, but David felt incredibly proud. He realized that fluency takes time, but consistency and a positive attitude make it possible.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Why did David feel embarrassed in Spanish class?
2.‎What advantage do adult learners have, according to the teacher?
3.‎What small victory did David experience a few months later?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:

He felt... ⁦(embarrassing)⁩ and thought he was too old to learn.
The teacher told him that adults have discipline and should focus on... ⁦(consistency)⁩.
She explained that making mistakes is how you... ⁦(practice)⁩.
David decided not to give up and listened to a... ⁦(podcast)⁩ at home.
He stopped worrying about his... ⁦(accent)⁩.
Months later, he successfully ordered coffee in Spanish, which showed his... ⁦(progress)⁩.
He learned that the teacher's support helped to... ⁦(encourage)⁩ him.

Тема: № 143 Преодоление страха публичных выступлений на местном мероприятии ⁦(Overcoming the fear of speaking at a local community event)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎public speaking [ˈpʌblɪk ˈspiːkɪŋ] — публичные выступления
2.‎community event [kəˈmjuːnəti ɪˈvent] — местное мероприятие
3.‎nervous [ˈnɜːvəs] — нервничающий
4.‎to prepare [prɪˈpeə] — готовиться
5.‎audience [ˈɔːdiəns] — аудитория, слушатели
6.‎to stumble [ˈstʌmbl] — запинаться, оговариваться
7.‎confident [ˈkɒnfɪdənt] — уверенный
8.‎applause [əˈplɔːz] — аплодисменты

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
Lisa stood backstage at the local library, her hands shaking. She had agreed to give a short talk about her experience starting a community garden, but now she was terrified. 

"I can't do this," she whispered to her friend, Mark. "My mind is blank. I’m going to stumble over every word."

Mark handed her a bottle of water. "You’ve prepared for this for two weeks. You know this topic better than anyone in that room. Just breathe."

"But what if the audience thinks I’m boring?" 

"Then they’ll be bored for five minutes, and then you’ll be done," Mark joked gently. "But they won’t be. They are here because they care about the garden, just like you."

The organizer called her name. Lisa walked onto the small stage. The lights were bright, and about thirty people were looking at her. 

"Hello," she began, her voice a little shaky. "My name is Lisa, and I want to share how our small garden grew."

She looked down at her notes, then looked up and made eye contact with a friendly woman in the front row. She started talking about the first seeds they planted. 

Halfway through, she lost her train of thought. She paused, took a deep breath, and smiled. "Sorry, I got a bit nervous. Let me start that sentence again." 

To her surprise, the audience smiled back warmly. No one judged her. She finished her talk, and when she said "Thank you," the room erupted in loud applause. 

Walking off the stage, Mark gave her a high-five. "See? You were great."

Lisa let out a long breath. She was still nervous, but she felt incredibly proud. She had faced her fear of public speaking and survived.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):

2.‎How did Mark help calm her down?
3.‎How did the audience react when Lisa stumbled over her words?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:

Lisa went on stage and made eye contact with the... ⁦(audience)⁩.
When she lost her train of thought, she paused and... ⁦(smiled)⁩.
The audience was supportive, and she finished her talk.
At the end, she received loud... ⁦(applause)⁩.

4.‎anxious [ˈæŋkʃəs] — тревожный
5.‎nature [ˈneɪtʃə] — природа

8.‎habit [ˈhæbɪt] — привычка

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
"Okay, it’s Friday at 6 PM," Sam said, placing his smartphone in a drawer and locking it. "No phone, no laptop, no TV until Monday morning."

His wife, Emma, looked at him skeptically. "A full weekend digital detox? Are you sure you can do it? You check your email every ten minutes."

"I need to unplug," Sam insisted. "I feel anxious all the time. I want to see if I can survive without constant notifications."

The first few hours were surprisingly hard. Sam kept reaching for his pocket out of habit. He felt a phantom vibration. Without his phone, the house felt too quiet. 

"Let’s go for a walk," Emma suggested, noticing his restlessness. "Let’s enjoy nature."

They walked to the nearby park. At first, Sam kept thinking about work emails and the news he was missing. But as they walked further, he started noticing things: the sound of birds, the color of the autumn leaves, and the fresh air. 

"Look at that dog," Emma pointed out. 

Sam smiled. "It’s a golden retriever. I haven't really looked at a dog in years without taking a photo of it first."

They spent the afternoon reading physical books, cooking a meal together, and just talking. There were no interruptions, no screens, and no distractions. 

By Sunday evening, Sam felt completely different. He wasn't anxious anymore. His mind felt clear, and he felt truly recharged. 

"I can't believe how much time we waste on those screens," Sam admitted as he unlocked the drawer on Monday morning. "I’m not giving up my phone completely, but I think I’ll make this a regular habit. Maybe one day a week."

Emma smiled. "I’ll hold you to that. Welcome back to the real world."

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Why did Sam decide to do a digital detox for the weekend?
2.‎What was the hardest part of the first few hours?
3.‎How did Sam feel by Sunday evening?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Sam decided to... ⁦(unplug)⁩ from all devices for the weekend.
His wife Emma was skeptical because he checked his phone constantly for... ⁦(notifications)⁩.
At first, Sam felt... ⁦(anxious)⁩ and reached for his pocket out of... ⁦(habit)⁩.
Emma suggested they go for a walk in... ⁦(nature)⁩.
Sam started noticing the world around him and felt more... ⁦(present)⁩.
They spent the weekend reading, cooking, and talking without screens.
By Sunday, Sam felt completely... ⁦(recharged)⁩ and clear-minded.
He decided to make a screen-free day a regular... ⁦(habit)⁩.

Тема: № 145 Принятие сложного решения: переезд, смена работы или расставание ⁦(Making a tough life decision: moving, changing jobs, or breaking up)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎decision [dɪˈsɪʒn] — решение
2.‎to weigh up [weɪ ʌp] — взвешивать (за и против)
3.‎opportunity [ˌɒpəˈtjuːnəti] — возможность, шанс
4.‎risk [rɪsk] — риск
5.‎to support [səˈpɔːt] — поддерживать
6.‎uncertain [ʌnˈsɜːtn] — неопределенный, неуверенный
7.‎to regret [rɪˈɡret] — сожалеть
8.‎leap of faith [liːp əv feɪθ] — прыжок веры (решительный шаг)

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~280 слов.
Alex sat at the kitchen table, staring at the email on his laptop. It was a job offer from a tech company in Berlin. The salary was great, and the role was exactly what he wanted. But it meant leaving his current life, his friends, and his comfortable routine.

"You’ve been staring at that screen for an hour," his girlfriend, Mia, said, placing a cup of tea next to him. "Still weighing up the pros and cons?"

"Yes," Alex sighed. "It’s a huge opportunity, but it’s also a massive risk. What if I hate it? What if I regret leaving?"

Mia sat down opposite him. "It’s normal to feel uncertain. Big decisions are always scary. But let’s look at the facts. Are you happy in your current job?"

"Not really. I feel stuck," Alex admitted.

"Then staying is also a risk," Mia pointed out gently. "The risk of never knowing what could have been. I will support you whatever you choose. If you go, we can make long-distance work. If you stay, we’ll find a new hobby together."

Her words gave him clarity. He realized he was more afraid of regret than of failure. 

"Okay," Alex said, taking a deep breath. "I’m going to do it. I’m going to take this leap of faith."

Mia smiled and hugged him. "I’m proud of you. Let’s print that email and start planning."

A month later, Alex was packing his boxes. He was nervous, but also excited. He knew that making a tough life decision wasn't about having zero fear. It was about choosing the path that aligns with your goals, even when the future is uncertain.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎What difficult decision was Alex facing?
2.‎What argument did Mia use to help Alex see the situation differently?
3.‎How did Alex feel a month later when he was packing his boxes?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Alex received a great job... ⁦(opportunity)⁩ in Berlin.
He spent an hour... ⁦(weighing up)⁩ the pros and cons.
He was afraid of the... ⁦(risk)⁩ and felt... ⁦(uncertain)⁩ about the future.
Mia told him that staying in a job he dislikes is also a risk.
She promised to... ⁦(support)⁩ him whatever he chose.
Alex decided to take a... ⁦(leap of faith)⁩ and accept the offer.
A month later, he was packing, feeling nervous but excited.
He learned that making a tough... ⁦(decision)⁩ means choosing your goals over fear.

Тема: № 146 Ведение дневника благодарности и его влияние на настроение ⁦(Keeping a gratitude journal and its effect on mood)⁩
Уровень: A2-B1 ⁦(Elementary / Pre-Intermediate)⁩
1.‎Vocabulary (Словарь перед чтением)
Здесь всего 8 выражений. Они помогут понять текст, но не перегрузят память.
1.‎gratitude [ˈɡrætɪtjuːd] — благодарность
2.‎journal [ˈdʒɜːnl] — дневник, журнал
3.‎to reflect [rɪˈflekt] — размышлять, анализировать
4.‎mood [muːd] — настроение
5.‎positive [ˈpɒzətɪv] — позитивный
6.‎habit [ˈhæbɪt] — привычка
7.‎to appreciate [əˈpriːʃieɪt] — ценить, быть благодарным
8.‎mindset [ˈmaɪndset] — образ мыслей, настрой

2.‎The Story (История)
Текст с живыми диалогами и бытовыми деталями. Объем: ~275 слов.
Sarah felt constantly stressed. Between work deadlines and household chores, she always focused on what was going wrong. 

One evening, her friend Lisa noticed her complaining about the rain. "Sarah, you need to change your mindset," Lisa said. "Have you ever tried keeping a gratitude journal?"

"A what?" Sarah asked, skeptical. 

"A gratitude journal," Lisa explained. "Every night, before bed, you write down three small, positive things that happened during the day. It trains your brain to look for the good."

Sarah thought it sounded silly, but she was desperate. She bought a small notebook and a nice pen. 

That night, she sat on her bed and tried to reflect. "Okay, three things," she muttered. "One: the coffee this morning was really good. Two: I finished the report on time. Three: my cat slept on my lap."

She wrote them down. It felt a bit strange, but also calming. 

She continued this habit every night for a week. At first, it was hard to find things. But soon, she started noticing small joys during the day: a beautiful sunset, a funny text from a colleague, a delicious lunch. 

A week later, Lisa called her. "How’s the journal?"

"It’s actually working," Sarah admitted, smiling. "My mood is much better. I still have stress, but I appreciate the good moments more. I don't feel so overwhelmed."

"That’s the power of gratitude," Lisa said. "It doesn't change your life, but it changes how you see it."

Sarah looked at her journal on the nightstand. It was a simple habit, but it had made a huge difference in her daily life.

3.‎Retelling Practice (Практика пересказа)
Шаг 1. Ответь на простые вопросы (устно или письменно):
1.‎Why did Sarah feel constantly stressed at the beginning of the story?
2.‎What advice did Lisa give to Sarah?
3.‎How did Sarah’s mood change after a week of using the journal?

‎Шаг 2. Пересказ по плану ⁦(Fill in the blanks / Соедини идеи)⁩:
First, Sarah was always focused on negative things and felt... ⁦(stressed)⁩.
Lisa suggested she start keeping a... ⁦(gratitude)⁩ journal.
Every night, Sarah had to write down three... ⁦(positive)⁩ things.
At first, she thought it was silly, but she decided to try the... ⁦(habit)⁩.
She wrote about small things, like good coffee and her... ⁦(cat)⁩.
After a week, she started to... ⁦(appreciate)⁩ small joys during the day.
Her overall... ⁦(mood)⁩ improved, and she felt less overwhelmed.
She learned that this simple practice can change your... ⁦(mindset)⁩.
